### [87] [Higher-order rich clubs and configuration models on general directed hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01624)
*Jason P. Smith,Celia Hacker,Jānis Lazovskis,Florian Unger,Keith M. Smith,Daniela Egas Santander*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 提出了一种超富俱乐部（hyper-rich club）分析框架，用于检测复杂网络中高阶交互结构，适用于多种类型的有向超图，并在不同类型真实数据中发现了传统富俱乐部分析无法捕捉的有意义结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的富俱乐部分析仅考虑节点间的成对交互，忽略了高阶相互作用，而这些高阶结构在许多复杂系统中起关键作用。因此，需要一种能捕捉高阶和方向性信息的新方法。

Method: 构建了一个基于一般有向超图的超富俱乐部分析流程，统一了多种有向超图定义，并允许研究者根据需求选择具体实现方式，从而涵盖图、无向超图及各类有向超图上的富俱乐部效应。

Result: 在连接组、传染病传播时序网络、诗歌网络和XGI超图数据库等多种实际网络中验证了该方法的有效性，成功识别出标准图富俱乐部分析遗漏的结构性特征。

Conclusion: 超富俱乐部框架能够有效揭示包含方向性和高阶交互的复杂网络中的重要组织结构，扩展了传统富俱乐部分析的适用范围和表达能力。

Abstract: Detecting structure in complex networks, especially those arising from physical systems, is a central problem across the sciences. One approach is via rich club analysis, which identifies important vertices using a centrality metric and measures whether those vertices are more tightly interconnected than expected by chance. While informative, this approach captures only pairwise interactions, missing out on higher-order ones known to shape the structure and function of many complex systems. We propose a hyper-rich club pipeline that asks whether central vertices are more tightly interconnected than expected by chance through hyperedges encoding higher-order interactions, which also enables the inclusion of important, often omitted, directional information. We work in a broad class of hypergraphs, which we call general directed hypergraphs, that includes as special cases undirected hypergraphs, head-and-tail directed hypergraphs, and totally ordered hypergraphs (a hypergraph related to directed simplicial complexes from topological data analysis). This unifies several non-equivalent notions of directed hypergraph under one definition. On these hypergraphs we define a hyper-rich club framework whose concrete construction depends on explicit choices the domain scientist fixes according to their research goals. Particular choices recover the existing rich club notions for graphs and undirected hypergraphs, and yield the first such notion for each version of directed hypergraphs. We demonstrate that the pipeline recovers meaningful structure in data by studying networks of very different origins: connectomes, temporal networks of infectious spread, networks of poems, and the XGI hypergraph database, in each case detecting structure the standard graph rich club misses.

</details>


### [88] [Whose Judgments Count? Representation Gaps in Crowdsourced Content Moderation Produce Unequal Protection from Perceived Toxicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01625)
*Zhaodi Chen,Byungkyu Lee*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析16,221名美国用户对10万条社交媒体评论的审查判断，结合反事实模拟，揭示了内容审核中存在“群体内保护”现象：审核员的人口构成直接影响哪些用户受到保护，而当前代表性审核池仍导致黑人和LGB群体保护不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨内容审核系统中，审核人群的人口统计学构成如何影响不同用户群体受到的保护程度，揭示现有聚合机制可能带来的结构性不平等。

Method: 结合大规模判断数据与反事实模拟，分析来自Prolific平台的16,221名美国受访者对Twitter、Reddit和4chan上102,463条评论的毒性评估，比较不同构成的审核池对保护分布的影响。

Result: 发现存在显著的‘群体内保护’效应，即与审核员共享人口特征的用户更少暴露于被判定为有毒的内容；模拟显示，即使完全代表性的审核池也无法实现平等保护，黑人和LGB用户仍被保护不足，除非其在审核池中占比远超人口比例。

Conclusion: 在线内容审核中的不平等保护源于分层审核标准的聚合过程，审核员的人口构成是决定谁被保护的关键因素，单纯的人口代表性不足以纠正系统性偏差。

Abstract: Content moderation is a central form of digital governance, yet people disagree over what content should be removed from shared online spaces. While platforms aggregate human judgments to build moderation systems, it remains unclear how this process shapes which users are protected from content they perceive as toxic. We address this gap by combining large-scale judgment data with counterfactual simulations that trace how the demographic composition of moderator pools shapes the distribution of protection across users. Applying this framework to removal judgments from 16,221 U.S. respondents evaluating 102,463 comments from Twitter, Reddit, and 4chan, we find demographic heterogeneities in moderation demand. We further reveal a consistent pattern of in-group protection: reductions in perceived toxicity accrue disproportionately to users who share the demographic identities of the moderator pool. Crucially, moderator pools that mirror the demographic composition of self-identified moderators on Prolific widen these disparities relative to a nationally representative baseline, while even fully representative pools fail to ensure equal protection: Black and LGB users remain underprotected unless they are represented well beyond their population share. These findings show that unequal protection from perceived toxicity can arise structurally from the aggregation of stratified removal standards, making the demographic composition of moderation inputs a key determinant of who is protected online.

</details>


### [89] [Graph Neural Team Recommendation: An Integrated Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01631)
*Md Jamil Ahmed,Mahdis Saeedi,Hossein Fani*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的团队推荐方法，通过在专家合作图上进行链接预测，利用多跳团队内外协作信息，克服了传统两阶段方法忽略结构信息和无法联合优化的问题，在多个大规模数据集上实现了新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有团队推荐方法忽略了专家合作图中的关系和结构信息，且技能向量预训练与模型训练分离，导致无法充分捕捉专家间的复杂依赖关系并实现端到端优化。

Method: 将团队推荐问题重构为专家合作图上的端到端链接预测任务，利用图结构中的多跳协作信息（包括团队内和跨团队协作），实现联合优化。

Result: 在两个来自不同领域的大规模数据集上实验表明，所提方法优于现有方法，取得了新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 端到端的链接预测框架能更有效地建模专家协作结构，提升团队推荐效果，为未来研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Team recommendation aims to select an optimal subset of experts who can form an almost surely successful collaborative team for a given set of required skills. State-of-the-art methods are neural multi-label classifiers that transfer dense vector representations of skills into a sparse occurrence vector representing the optimal subset of experts. Such methods, however, overlook experts' relational and structural information encoded in the expert collaboration graph and, thus, fall short of capturing complex inter-dependencies among experts and their associated skills within teams. Moreover, the skills' dense vectors are pretrained disjointly and independently of the underlying neural classifier, hence, preventing end-to-end optimization. In this paper, we propose to reformulate the team recommendation problem into end-to-end link predictions in the expert collaboration graph to consume multi-hop intra-team and cross-team collaborations among experts while eschewing the unnecessary complexities of the disjoint two-phase training procedure. Our experiments on two large-scale datasets from various domains with distinct distributions of skills in teams demonstrate the superiority of the end-to-end approach and establish a new state of the art. Our code is available at https://github.com/fani-lab/OpeNTF.

</details>


### [90] [Omega-N: Interpretable Structural Node Descriptors and Their Applicability Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01633)
*Alberto Acedo*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部化三角形结构的新型网络特征Omega-N，通过谱分析和节点级归因，在无训练、无嵌入的情况下实现了可解释的节点分类，并在药物靶点预测中显著优于传统中心性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在开发一种可解释且非冗余的网络结构指数，克服全局标量指标在谱上的冗余问题，提升在未知结构中心数量场景下的节点分类性能。

Method: 通过对邻接矩阵三次方的对角线元素进行局部化处理，结合配置零模型 excess 和个性化PageRank多尺度邻域，构建包含十个可解释特征的Omega-N指标，仅依赖图结构信息。

Result: Omega-N在六项节点分类任务中一项胜出、四项持平；能准确划分八个基准测试中的六项；在蛋白质相互作用网络的药物靶点优先排序中，AUPRC提升0.073至0.144，经独立数据验证并通过多种偏倚控制。

Conclusion: Omega-N作为一种无需训练、具有可解释性的特征方法，在特定任务尤其是药物靶点识别中优于传统中心性指标，但当与Node2Vec等嵌入方法结合时增益不显著，适用范围有限。

Abstract: A composite structural index summarises a network in one number; for a triangle-based index it is spectrally redundant: Tr(A^3) is the third moment of the adjacency spectrum. The non-redundant content sits one level down, in diag(A^3), which depends on eigenvectors and is not spectrally determined. A corollary in the theory paper for this index family stated that, and predicted: the global scalar should tie sharpened spectral baselines rather than beat them, while the node-wise attribution should do better where the number of structural epicentres is unknown. This paper tests it.
  We construct Omega-N by localizing each of the four factors. The direct localization is badly conditioned; two corrections from published practice fix it, a configuration-null excess for every local factor and a personalized-PageRank neighbourhood at several scales, giving ten interpretable features per node from the graph alone, with no attributes, training or embeddings.
  Against a recursive feature engine at five levels of recursion, Omega-N wins on one and ties on four of six in-domain node-classification evaluations, with ten features against up to 252. Two statistics computed from the graph and labels, not from performance, partition the eight benchmarks without error, and the two they exclude are the two it loses.
  The strongest application is drug-target prioritisation on protein interaction networks: +0.073 to +0.144 AUPRC over a centrality battery across four constructions, replicated on an independent AP-MS network and label source, surviving three bias controls (degree-matched, ten repetitions: +0.1047 and +0.1030, both 10/10, p=0.00195). The clearest negative sits in the same application: adding Omega-N to centralities plus Node2Vec changes nothing (+0.0014, p=0.31). The claim is narrow: ten interpretable features

</details>


### [91] [Discretization-free exact recovery in geometric community detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01635)
*Maarten Hoeneveld,Moritz Otto,Raphaël Sala*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需离散化的多项式时间算法，用于几何隐藏社区模型中的精确社区恢复，能够在较弱条件下实现精确检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖空间离散化，限制了连接函数的灵活性，难以处理复杂的空间与社区联合结构。

Method: 设计了一种直接在连续几何空间上操作的算法，不依赖离散化，利用社区间连接模式的差异进行精确恢复。

Result: 算法在连接函数部分重叠、且社区仅通过第三方社区连接才能区分的情况下仍能实现精确恢复；实验表明其性能超出理论保证范围。

Conclusion: 该方法放宽了传统模型假设，提升了几何社区检测的适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Geometric community detection seeks to recover latent communities in networks where connectivity depends jointly on community structure and continuous spatial geometry. Existing exact-recovery approaches typically discretize the underlying space, which can impose restrictive structural assumptions on the connectivity functions. We develop a polynomial-time, discretization-free algorithm for exact recovery in the Geometric Hidden Community Model (GHCM), operating directly on the continuous geometry. Our method succeeds even when connectivity functions coincide on a nontrivial portion of their visibility range and when two communities can be distinguished only through their connectivity to a third community. We prove exact recovery under these weaker conditions and provide experiments showing that the algorithm succeeds beyond the theoretically guaranteed regime.

</details>


### [92] [A Novel Information Workflow for Structural Behavioural Analysis in Dynamic Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01640)
*Gatadi Ashwitha,K. Swarupa Rani*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于属性图的增量链接预测与社区检测（inc-LPCDAG）信息工作流，以动态分析社交系统中的结构与行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理社交系统的动态性方面存在局限，且无法同时识别属性图中的潜在链接并形成紧密连接的社区。

Method: 结合链接预测、社区检测与基于交互的分析，利用属性与结构信息，提出inc-LPCDAG工作流，支持增量式处理。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在效率和有效性方面表现良好，能够更好地分析社交系统的结构与行为演化。

Conclusion: inc-LPCDAG为属性图上的动态社会系统分析提供了有效解决方案，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Social systems play a vital role through interactions around shared interests and the maintenance of relationships, which are part of society. These can be represented as networks, and their analysis plays a crucial role in dynamic environments, which can be achieved through link prediction and community detection tasks. Several link prediction approaches estimate new relationships, such as suggesting friends on social media. On the other hand, a social faction is a group of connected people that reveals the internal structure of the social system and can be identified through community detection approaches. The evolution of social systems can be understood using these approaches by analysing their structure and behaviour. To handle this, a combination of these approaches, along with interaction-based analysis, is used to form cohesive communities, thereby enabling the study of social systems dynamically. In addition, attribute information plays a critical role. Most real-world graphs, like Facebook and Twitter, have attribute information that provides context, and integrating them with structural information offers deeper insights into patterns, which is a critical task. However, existing approaches have limitations in handling dynamics of social systems. Additionally, no existing approaches in the literature help identify potential links and form densely connected communities for attributed graphs. To address these challenges, we have proposed an information workflow, i.e., inc-LPCDAG (\underline{inc}remental Link Prediction and Community Detection in Attributed Graphs). We conducted experiments to evaluate efficiency, and results demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed workflow by analysing and understanding structural and behavioural processes.

</details>


### [93] [SocialBuddy: Tailoring Search Agent for Social Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01641)
*Mingxuan Li,Yirong Mao,FaZhan Zhang,Haibiao Yao,Runze Hu,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SocialBuddy，首个面向社交场景的智能搜索框架，通过构建大规模模拟环境SocialEnv和设计混合粒度优化框架SocialPO，显著提升了在复杂社交流中检索好友帖子的效果，并建立了SocialSearch基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能搜索框架在处理异构用户查询和多维社交信息流时表现不佳，难以满足数字社交时代用户对高效社交搜索的需求。

Method: 提出SocialBuddy框架，构建包含20万用户、1000万帖子和5万推理轨迹的大规模模拟环境SocialEnv；设计结合多维度奖励与细粒度前缀截断、词元级监督的混合优化框架SocialPO；并建立SocialSearch基准用于量化评估。

Result: 实验表明，SocialBuddy-35B在社交搜索任务上显著优于更大规模的前沿大语言模型。

Conclusion: SocialBuddy为复杂社交搜索提供了有效解决方案，其混合粒度优化策略和仿真环境为未来社交智能检索系统的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the era of digital social interaction, searching friends' posts from massive social streams has become a fundamental user need. However, while modern agentic search frameworks have achieved remarkable success in conventional retrieval tasks, they break down when confronted with heterogeneous user queries and multi-dimensional social feeds, resulting in severe performance degradation in complex social search. To bridge this gap, we introduce SocialBuddy, the first agentic search framework tailored for social scenarios. Specifically, we construct SocialEnv, the first large-scale simulated environment for social search. Powered by an automated data and trajectory synthesis pipeline, SocialEnv includes 200K user profiles, 10 million social posts, and 50K reasoning trajectories, establishing a solid foundation for the development of social search agents. To tackle the credit assignment dilemma caused by sparse rewards in social search, we design SocialPO, a hybrid-granularity optimization framework. It macroscopically reinforces successful reasoning paths via multi-dimensional rewards, while microscopically rectifying deviated trajectories through fine-grained prefix truncation and token-level supervision. This hybrid-granularity design delivers multi-scale guidance in complex long-sequence scenarios. Finally, we construct SocialSearch Benchmark to provide a quantitative evaluation scheme for assessing the social search capabilities of SocialBuddy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SocialBuddy-35B surpasses significantly larger frontier LLMs. Code and dataset will be released upon article acceptance.

</details>


### [94] [A Data-Driven Multimodal Method for Early Detection of Coordinated Abnormal Behaviors in Live-Streaming Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01649)
*Jingwen Luo,Pinrui Zhu,Yiyan Wang,Zilin Xiao,Jingqi Li,Xuebei Kong,Yan Zhan*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MM-FGDNet的多模态框架，用于在大规模直播电商环境中检测跨模态、有组织的异常营销行为，结合时序演化和群体结构视角，实现了高精度与早期预警能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着直播电商和数字营销的快速发展，异常营销行为日益隐蔽且跨模态协同，对平台治理和早期风险识别构成挑战，亟需有效的检测方法。

Method: 提出MM-FGDNet框架：通过跨模态时序对齐模块将视频、文本、音频和用户行为映射到统一的时序语义空间；设计时序欺诈模式模块捕捉从弱信号到突发异常的演变过程；引入协同操纵模块识别用户群体与自动化账号的协作行为。

Result: 在真实多平台直播电商数据集上，MM-FGDNet优于现有基线模型，AUC达0.927，F1为0.847，精确率为0.861，召回率为0.834，早期检测得分为0.689，并降低了误报率；消融实验验证各模块有效性，跨域实验显示其对新主播、品类和平台具有稳定泛化能力。

Conclusion: MM-FGDNet能有效、可扩展地实现直播系统中协同异常行为的主动检测，具备良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of live-streaming e-commerce and digital marketing, abnormal marketing behaviors have become increasingly concealed and coordinated across heterogeneous modalities, challenging platform governance and early risk identification. We propose MM-FGDNet, a data-driven multimodal framework for detecting abnormal behavior in large-scale live-streaming environments from complementary temporal-evolution and group-structure perspectives. A cross-modal temporal alignment module maps video, text, audio, and user behavior into a unified temporal semantic space. A temporal fraud-pattern module captures the progression from weak early signals to abrupt outbreaks, while a cooperative manipulation module identifies coordinated interactions among organized user groups and automated accounts. Experiments on real-world multi-platform live-streaming e-commerce datasets show that MM-FGDNet outperforms representative baselines, achieving an AUC of 0.927, F1 of 0.847, precision of 0.861, recall of 0.834, and an Early Detection Score of 0.689, while reducing false alarms. Ablation studies validate the contribution of each module, and cross-domain experiments demonstrate stable generalization to new streamers, product categories, and platforms. These results indicate that MM-FGDNet provides an effective and scalable solution for proactive detection of coordinated abnormal behavior in live-streaming systems.

</details>


### [95] [From Detection to Characterization: A Large-Scale Study of Ragebait on Japanese X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02262)
*Zhiyang Qi,Kazuhiro Ito,Jinghui Chen,Hibiki Nakamura,Zhangxuan Chen,Erina Murata,Masaki Chujyo,Fujio Toriumi*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于大语言模型构建数据集并训练日语语言模型的集合分类器，用于大规模检测和分析日本社交媒体X上的愤怒诱饵内容。结果显示，愤怒诱饵多涉及政治和社会争议话题，传播更快且引发更多负面情绪反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对愤怒诱饵的大规模检测与系统性分析不足，限制了对其普遍性、影响及缓解措施的理解，因此需要开发有效的检测框架以深入研究此类煽动性在线内容。

Method: 利用大语言模型辅助构建标注数据集，训练多个日语语言模型，并通过集成学习方法建立愤怒诱饵检测器，随后将其应用于日本语言的X平台大规模帖子数据进行分析。

Result: 愤怒诱饵在政治、歧视、公共卫生和人际冲突等争议性话题中更为常见；与非愤怒诱饵相比，其传播速度更快，更易引发愤怒、恐惧、厌恶、悲伤和惊讶等负面情绪反应。

Conclusion: 所提出的检测框架有效支持了对日语网络话语中愤怒诱饵的大规模识别与特征刻画，为理解和应对煽动性内容提供了基础。

Abstract: Ragebait refers to online content intentionally designed to provoke anger or outrage and thereby increase attention and engagement. However, reliable large-scale detection and systematic analysis of ragebait remain limited, hindering efforts to understand its prevalence, impact, and mitigation. This study aims to develop an effective ragebait detection framework and to clarify the characteristics of ragebait at scale, providing a basis for understanding and mitigating emotionally provocative content online. We constructed a labeled dataset with the assistance of a large language model (LLM) and trained several Japanese language models for ragebait detection. The resulting ensemble classifier was then applied to a large-scale dataset of Japanese-language posts on X. Our analysis shows that ragebait is more prevalent in politically and socially contentious topics, including politics, discrimination, public health, and interpersonal conflict. Ragebait posts also spread faster and receive more negative reactions than non-ragebait posts, particularly anger, fear, disgust, sadness, and surprise. These findings demonstrate the utility of the proposed detector and provide a large-scale characterization of ragebait in Japanese online discourse.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Simple Covariate Adjustment for Many Estimands Using Stable Balancing Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01638)
*Kayla Irish,José Zubizarreta,Alex Luedtke*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种使用稳定平衡权重的简化协变量调整方法，适用于多种临床试验估计量，提升估计效率且易于实施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管协变量调整能提高临床试验中治疗效应估计的精度，但其对高级统计技术的需求限制了实际应用。因此需要一种通用且简便的方法来促进其广泛应用。

Method: 引入基于稳定平衡权重的协变量调整方法，适用于任何Hadamard可微分的估计量，并可通过支持观测权重的常规软件实现调整估计。

Result: 该方法在保持插件关系的同时，相较于未调整估计具有更高的渐近效率，且适用于多种常见估计量。

Conclusion: 所提出的简化协变量调整方法通用性强、效率高，便于在实际临床试验分析中推广使用。

Abstract: Covariate adjustment can improve precision in estimating treatment effects in clinical trials, and regulatory guidance increasingly encourages its implementation. Despite its benefits, covariate adjustment can require advanced statistical techniques or tailored approaches for different estimands, which can deter its use in practice. To address this issue, we introduce a simplified approach to covariate adjustment using stable balancing weights that applies directly across many clinical trial estimands, including the average treatment effect, relative risk, Mann-Whitney estimand, and survival ratio. More precisely, our results cover any estimand that is a Hadamard differentiable functional of the arm-specific distributions. Once the weights are obtained, our adjusted estimator can be implemented with the same software used for an unadjusted analysis, provided that software can take observation weights. This construction improves asymptotic efficiency relative to unadjusted estimation and preserves the plug-in relationship between arm-specific marginal and subgroup-specific summaries.

</details>


### [97] [Matrix-Aware Proper Scoring Rules and Significance Testing for Correlation and Covariance Forecasts in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01648)
*Vinh Nguyen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: corrscore是一个Python包，用于正确评估相关性或协方差矩阵的预测，提供适当的评分规则、无信息泄露的回测框架和显著性检验工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在评估相关性或协方差矩阵预测时存在缺陷，如使用不合适的评分指标、回测窗口重叠导致信息泄露、以及对序列依赖误差进行显著性检验的复杂性。

Method: 开发了corrscore Python包，实现了能量得分和变差得分等适当的评分规则，支持从解析解到蒙特卡洛抽样的多种预测类型，并设计了零重叠的回测框架和集成的显著性检验套件。

Result: 提供了矩阵感知的评分方法、几何感知的距离度量、无信息泄露的回测机制和开箱即用的显著性检验功能。

Conclusion: corrscore为风险管理和投资组合构建中的矩阵预测评估提供了可靠、易用且统计严谨的工具。

Abstract: Forecasting a correlation or covariance matrix is common in risk management and portfolio construction, but evaluating such a forecast correctly is not routine: naive matrix-comparison metrics are not proper scoring rules, walk-forward evaluation windows are easy to overlap with the estimation window in ways that silently leak information, and significance testing on serially dependent forecast-error sequences needs machinery few analysts implement from scratch. corrscore is a Python package that provides matrix-aware implementations of two established proper scoring rules for this setting -- the energy score and the variogram score -- dispatched across a closed-form tractability spectrum (point, discrete-mixture, and isotropic-Gaussian-mixture forecasts are scored exactly; a general Monte Carlo ensemble falls back to sampling), a geometry-aware variant of the variogram score built from the affine-invariant distance on the correlation manifold, a zero-overlap-by-construction walk-forward backtest harness, and a bundled significance-testing suite (circular block bootstrap, the Diebold-Mariano test, and the Model Confidence Set). We describe the package's design, its point of departure from the existing scoringRules and properscoring packages, and walk through a complete worked example.

</details>


### [98] [A normal-inverse-Wishart (NIW) Bayesian synthesizer for multivariate normal data with application to polygenic risk scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01656)
*Rasmus Rask Kragh Jørgensen,Anne Krogh Nøhr,Jan Reiter Sørensen,Martin Bøgsted,Heidi Søgaard Christensen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于共轭先验的贝叶斯合成方法，用于生成多元正态数据的合成数据集，并分析了在不同数据设置下属性披露风险的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对特征维度、原始数据集中个体数量以及发布合成信息量如何影响属性披露风险的理解尚不充分，需要一个可数学分析的方法来研究这些问题。

Method: 采用共轭正态-逆威沙特先验建模均值向量和协方差矩阵，推导出闭式后验分布，构建贝叶斯合成器，并通过模拟分析不同设置下对手推断记录的能力。

Result: 披露风险随原始数据集规模增大而降低，随特征空间维度和发布的合成信息量（如更大的合成数据集或多轮生成）增加而上升；在多基因风险评分数据集上的应用显示合成数据具有与原始数据相似的分布特性。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯合成器为理解合成数据中的隐私风险提供了理论框架，有助于在隐私保护与数据可用性之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Bayesian synthesis, which generates synthetic data by sampling from the posterior predictive distribution, is a popular approach for privatizing sensitive personal data. However, how attribute disclosure risk is affected by feature dimensionality, the number of individuals in the original dataset, and the amount of released synthetic information remains poorly understood. We propose a mathematically tractable Bayesian synthesizer for multivariate normal data based on a conjugate normal-inverse-Wishart prior for the mean vector and covariance matrix. The conjugate structure yields closed-form posteriors and enables direct investigation of an adversary's ability to infer records under different data and release settings. We then demonstrate several intuitive properties of synthetic data generation through several simulations. Specifically, we show that disclosure risk decreases with the size of the original dataset, but increases with the dimensionality of the feature space and the amount of synthetic information released, whether through the release of larger synthetic datasets or multiple generator realizations. Finally, the proposed synthesizer was used to generate synthetic versions of a polygenic risk score dataset, with the synthetic data exhibiting distributional properties comparable to those of the original data.

</details>


### [99] [On the discretization of the object space in inverse problems with application to cryo-electron microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01688)
*Gilles Mordant,Luke Evans,David Silva-Sánchez,Pilar Cossio,Roy Lederman*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了在低温电子显微镜（cryo-EM）中从噪声观测中恢复概率分布的去卷积问题，分析了离散化与噪声对权重估计的影响，提供了统计与算法层面的理论结果，并通过Hsp90分子的合成图像验证了理论发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多逆问题中，目标是从间接且含噪的观测中恢复潜在空间上的概率分布。特别是在cryo-EM中，需估计生物分子构象的相对频率，因此需要理解离散化和噪声对估计性能的影响。

Method: 将问题建模为概率测度空间上的去卷积问题，采用有限候选点集并估计其权重，转化为单纯形上的有限维凹最大似然问题；分析权重恢复问题，结合KL散度下界、渐近正态性及边界情况下的极限分布，并利用EM算法的精确邻近形式进行全局高噪声比较。

Result: 证明了候选点间距与噪声水平共同决定了可观测密度间KL散度的统一下界；在内部目标下估计量渐近正态，在边界目标下收敛于锥投影高斯分布；EM早期迭代可与KL惩罚似然进行无基盆假设的全局比较。

Conclusion: 离散化网格与噪声水平显著影响构象权重估计的精度与统计性质，研究结果为解释重加权系综提供了理论依据和实用指南。

Abstract: In many inverse problems, the aim is to recover a probability distribution on a latent (or object state) space from indirect, noisy observations. When the observations can be modelled as noisy samples from the mixing latent distribution, the recovery problem is a deconvolution problem on the space of probability measures. A common strategy is to fix a finite set of candidate points and estimate a weight for each, turning the problem into a finite-dimensional concave maximum likelihood problem on the simplex. We study the combined effect of this discretization and of the noise in the context of cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM), where the candidate points are biomolecular conformations and the weights describe the relative frequency of each conformation. Our results pertain to both statistical and algorithmic aspects of the estimator. We analyze the weight-recovery problem in which the candidate states and their likelihoods are known. A nearby pair of candidates forces a near-null direction. More generally, the grid and the noise level impose a uniform lower bound on the achievable Kullback--Leibler divergence between observation densities, even with infinite data. The finite-grid estimator is asymptotically normal when its population target is in the interior of the simplex; at a boundary target, its limit is a cone-projected Gaussian. Finally, the exact proximal form of Expectation--Maximization leads to a global high-noise comparison between an early iterate and a KL-penalized likelihood, without a basin assumption or a linearization of the recursion. Tests on synthetic images of the Hsp90 molecule illustrate the theoretical findings and translate them into practical guidelines for interpreting reweighted ensembles.

</details>


### [100] [Robust Bayesian Inference for Unnormalized Models with Mixed-Domain Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01783)
*Jiongran Wang,Debdeep Pati,Anirban Bhattacharya*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SME-BETEL的半参数贝叶斯框架，结合得分匹配估计方程与贝叶斯指数倾斜经验似然，避免了归一化常数的计算，实现了在模型误设下仍具有有效频率覆盖的可信集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多统计模型中的参数依赖归一化常数难以计算，阻碍了标准贝叶斯推断；现有方法在模型误设时不确定性量化可能不准确。因此需要一种无需计算归一化常数且在误设下仍具良好校准性的贝叶斯方法。

Method: 将得分匹配估计方程与贝叶斯指数倾斜经验似然（BETEL）相结合，构建SME-BETEL框架；理论证明其后验具有一致性、渐近正态性和Bernstein-von Mises性质；提出适用于混合域数据的新得分匹配准则，扩展至双难处理模型。

Result: SME-BETEL无需计算归一化常数和学习率调参；理论证明其可信集在模型误设下仍具渐近有效的频率覆盖；新准则支持混合域数据（如空间偏好抽样模型）；模拟显示其在误设下显著改善不确定性量化；臭氧监测应用验证了实际效用。

Conclusion: SME-BETEL为双难处理模型提供了一种稳健的贝叶斯推断方法，在无需计算归一化常数的同时，保证了在模型误设下的有效不确定性量化和频率覆盖，尤其适用于混合域数据。

Abstract: Many statistical models involve parameter-dependent normalizing constants that are computationally intractable, creating substantial obstacles to standard Bayesian inference. Although existing likelihood-based algorithms can often circumvent these constants, their uncertainty quantification may be poorly calibrated under model misspecification. To address these challenges, we propose SME-BETEL, a semiparametric Bayesian framework that combines score matching estimating equations with Bayesian exponentially tilted empirical likelihood. The resulting posterior avoids evaluation of normalizing constants and does not require learning-rate calibration. We establish consistency and asymptotic normality of the score matching estimator, and prove a Bernstein-von Mises theorem for the SME-BETEL posterior. These results show that SME-BETEL credible sets are asymptotically calibrated to the sampling variability of the score matching estimator, yielding valid frequentist coverage under model misspecification. We further develop a new score matching criterion for mixed-domain data, extending SME-BETEL to models whose observations combine components from different sample spaces. This construction enables robust Bayesian inference for mixed-domain doubly-intractable models, including preferential sampling models with an intractable spatial normalizing integral. Simulation studies show that SME-BETEL remains competitive under correct specification and substantially improves uncertainty quantification under misspecification. An ozone-monitoring application further demonstrates the utility of the mixed-domain construction for spatial preferential modeling.

</details>


### [101] [Conditional Distribution Estimation Given Functional Covariates Using Deep Operator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01842)
*Bingqing Hu,Ran Zou,Bin Nan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的标量响应与函数协变量回归方法，通过深度算子网络和卷积神经网络估计条件分布函数，而非仅限于条件均值，并在模拟和实际数据中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的函数线性模型主要关注条件均值的建模，难以捕捉复杂数据下的完整条件分布信息，且受限于函数线性假设。

Method: 采用基于似然的条件风险函数方法，结合卷积神经网络与深度算子网络，对函数输入的影响进行非参数逼近，以估计完整的条件分布函数。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据实验中，所提方法相比均值回归神经网络展现出更强的鲁棒性，在条件分布估计和区间预测方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了传统函数线性模型的限制，提供了更灵活、准确的函数型数据分析工具，适用于复杂数据结构下的统计推断。

Abstract: Functional linear models are commonly used for analyzing functional data with scalar responses, particularly for modeling the conditional mean of the response given functional covariates. In this work, we extend the traditional scalar-on-function regression in two major aspects: 1. estimating the conditional distribution function instead of a particular characteristic such as the conditional mean; 2. considering an arbitrary operator without imposing functional linearity or any model assumption. We use a likelihood approach for the conditional hazard function and apply convolutional neural networks to approximate the effects of functional inputs and estimate the conditional distribution using deep operator networks. Through simulations and a real world data example, we show the desirable robustness of the proposed method in comparison with the mean regression neural networks, demonstrating that our approach achieves better conditional distribution estimation and interval prediction in complex data settings.

</details>


### [102] [Statistical Inference for Probability Barycenters and Kolmogorov Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02869)
*Manuela-Simona Cojocea*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率坐标图的概率推断框架，通过将观测值映射到单位区间进行平均，并研究在固定、内在和估计坐标图下的矩量推断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计推断在非线性尺度上难以直接平均或定义矩量，因此需要一种能保持几何结构且适用于不同坐标系统的统一推断方法。

Method: 引入概率坐标图作为严格递增的双射函数，将观测映射到单位区间，在该区间计算坐标矩，并通过逆映射回原始尺度；分别处理固定、内在和估计的坐标图情形，发展相应的置信区间、渐近展开与重采样方法。

Result: 对于固定坐标图，提出了保持几何形状的置信区间并给出分布无关的有限样本界；内在情形退化为中位数推断；对于估计的位置-尺度坐标图，提出了校准修正的渐近展开、影响函数方差估计及重校准自助法，并建立了联合协方差理论。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了不同类型坐标图下的统计推断，分离了概率尺度变异、逆图放大效应与坐标图校准不确定性，为基于矩的推断提供了灵活且稳健的方法。

Abstract: A probability coordinate chart is a continuous strictly increasing bijection that transports observations to the open unit interval, where averaging is always well defined. For barycentric inference, the first coordinate moment is returned through the inverse chart. Higher initial coordinate moments similarly generate initial Kolmogorov moments by pullback, while centred coordinate moments remain on the probability scale. We develop inference for these quantities under fixed, intrinsic, and estimated charts.
  For a fixed benchmark chart, the coordinate mean is the primary inferential object. Confidence intervals are constructed on the probability scale and then transported through the inverse chart, preserving the geometry and allowing asymmetry on the observation scale. Hoeffding's inequality also provides finite-sample distribution-free intervals.
  The intrinsic case requires different treatment. The empirical cumulative distribution function is not an admissible chart, and its natural generalised plug-in construction collapses exactly to a middle order statistic. Feasible intrinsic inference therefore reduces to median inference. This self-induced functional is distinguished from the frozen oracle construction, which has a different asymptotic variance.
  For estimated location-scale charts, the asymptotic expansion contains a calibration correction for the first-order effect of learning the chart from the same sample. This yields an influence-function variance estimator and a bootstrap procedure that recalibrates the chart in every resample. Joint covariance theory is developed for vectors of initial and centred coordinate moments, with initial Kolmogorov moments obtained by inverse-chart pullback. The framework separates variation on the probability scale, amplification by the inverse chart, and uncertainty from chart calibration.

</details>


### [103] [Uncertainty Quantification of State Variables Trajectories in the Context of Inverse Problems: An Approach from Bayesian Inference and FDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01919)
*Luis Alejandro Baena-Marín,Juan Daniel Molina,Juan Camilo Bermúdez-Colorado,Nicolás Moreno*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种结合贝叶斯推断、哈密尔顿蒙特卡洛采样和函数数据分析的不确定性量化方法，用于逆问题中状态变量轨迹的可信区域构建，验证显示该方法优于传统逐点分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在逆问题中对状态变量不确定性传播的量化能力有限，仅能构建伪可信区域或在孤立点上量化不确定性，缺乏对整个时间范围内轨迹的全面刻画。

Method: 结合贝叶斯推断与哈密尔顿蒙特卡洛采样进行后验分布高效探索，并引入函数数据分析中的Modified Band Depth方法，以确定状态变量在整个时间范围内的可信区域。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提方法生成的可信区域包含真实轨迹的比例达到96.4%，显著高于传统逐点方法的80%；并在一个与神经现象相关的非平凡模型中成功应用，有效捕捉了状态变量的时间依赖动态。

Conclusion: 所提出的融合贝叶斯与函数数据分析的方法能更准确地量化逆问题中状态变量的不确定性，提供更可靠的全局可信区域，具有较强的实用性和推广价值。

Abstract: In this article, we address the problem of uncertainty quantification of state variables in the context of inverse problems. Inverse problems are associated with phenomena that can be represented through ordinary or partial differential equations, for which observations or data are available, but the values of the parameters that characterize the equations, the initial or boundary conditions are not known. Currently, the literature offers very few alternatives for analyze the propagation of uncertainty of state variables, which are limited to constructing pseudo-credible regions or quantifying their uncertainty at isolated points. We propose a methodology that combines tools of Bayesian inference, with Hamiltonian Monte Carlo sampling employed for efficient posterior exploration, and functional data analysis, specifically the Modified Band Depth method, to determine credible regions for the trajectories of the state variables throughout the time horizon of interest. We expose a methodology validation through a simulation study, which shows that our proposal captures a higher proportion of state variable trajectories than traditional pointwise analysis methods, our proposal generated credible regions that contained the true trajectory of the state variables 96.4\% of the times, versus 80\% that the regions of the pointwise method did. Furthermore, we demonstrate its application to a non-trivial model associated with a neurological phenomenon, for which the methodology effectively captures the time-dependent dynamics of the state variables.

</details>


### [104] [Bigraphical Matérn-Whittle (BMW) Processes for Fast Inference of Big Multivariate Spatial Data on General Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01950)
*Debangan Dey,Alokesh Manna,Christopher J. Geoga*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双图Mat'ern-Whittle过程，用于大规模空间多变量数据建模，通过空间图和变量图联合建模变量间的依赖关系，并保持变量级解释性，具有良好的可扩展性和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在复杂拓扑上同时建模多变量空间依赖并保持变量可解释性，尤其当欧氏距离不能准确表示邻近关系时。

Method: 引入双图Mat'ern-Whittle过程：利用图拉普拉斯的分数次幂构建每个变量的空间Mat'ern结构（空间图），并通过有向无环变量图编码变量间科学结构，实现条件独立性建模；采用稀疏矩阵运算实现高效推断。

Result: 该方法在模拟中准确恢复参数和图结构，对误设具有鲁棒性，在非凸域上预测误差减半；在包含19,809个细胞和1,122个基因的空间转录组数据上，75分钟内完成拟合，利用学习到的基因图使预测误差降低50%至91%。

Conclusion: 双图框架有效解决了大规模空间多变量联合建模的挑战，支持复杂拓扑、变量间条件独立表达，并实现可扩展的贝叶斯学习与预测。

Abstract: Large spatial data sets now record many correlated variables at many thousands of locations, often on domains where Euclidean distance misrepresents proximity. The central difficulty is modelling the cross-variable dependence jointly while retaining variable-level interpretation. We introduce the bigraphical Mat'ern-Whittle process, a multivariate Gaussian process that resolves this with two graphs. A spatial graph generates the Mat'ern structure of each variable through a fractional power of a graph Laplacian, so the process is valid on any topology, with per-variable range, smoothness and amplitude. A directed acyclic variable graph encodes the scientific structure: we prove that each absent edge yields an exact conditional independence between the corresponding fields. We further prove that the operator determinant does not involve the cross-dependence coefficients, which keeps matrix-free likelihood evaluation and Bayesian learning of the variable graph tractable at scale. Estimation requires only sparse matrix-vector products and scales to tens of millions of space-variable pairs. In simulations the method recovered parameters and graphs accurately, remained robust under misspecification, and halved held-out prediction error on a non-convex domain. In a spatial transcriptomics section with 19,809 cells and 1,122 genes, fitted in 75 minutes on a laptop, borrowing across the learned gene graph reduced held-out prediction error by 50 to 91 percent. Theoretical challenges, such as the achievable efficiency of estimating the variance of the nugget, are also explored.

</details>


### [105] [A staggered seamless dose-optimization design for co-developing monotherapy and combination therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01954)
*Masahiro Kojima,Kentaro Takeda,Ying Yuan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种分阶段无缝I/II期设计，用于在单药和联合治疗中优化新药剂量，通过模型辅助规则和贝叶斯边界提高效率和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在评估新药单药及联合治疗时，平衡疗效、活性和耐受性，提高剂量优化的效率。

Method: 采用分阶段无缝I/II期设计，I期先进行单药剂量探索，根据早期信号自适应开启联合治疗亚组，使用基于毒性和早期疗效的模型辅助规则进行剂量分配，并在II期使用贝叶斯无效和疗效边界进行最终最优生物剂量选择。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该设计相比对照设计缩短了I期试验时间，保持了良好的最优生物剂量选择性能和可接受的安全性，同时减少了II期额外入组需求。

Conclusion: 该无缝设计能有效提升肿瘤药物开发中剂量优化的效率与安全性，支持更快速、精准地确定推荐剂量。

Abstract: Contemporary oncology drug development increasingly requires efficient dose-optimization strategies that evaluate monotherapy (Mono) and combination therapy (Combo) while balancing activity, efficacy, and tolerability. We propose a staggered seamless phase I/II design for settings in which a novel agent is evaluated alone and in combination with an established therapy. In phase I, Mono dose finding begins first, and Combo subtrials can be opened adaptively once a prespecified combination-initiation signal based on early clinical or biological information is observed. Dose assignment uses a model-assisted rule based on toxicity and early activity, with backfilling at tolerable and potentially promising regimens. At the end of phase I, two candidate regimens are selected from the evaluated Mono and Combo regimens using an efficacy-toxicity utility based on accumulated toxicity and treatment-response data. Phase II seamlessly carries forward patients treated at the selected regimens, enrolls additional patients as needed, and applies Bayesian futility and efficacy stopping boundaries to identify a final recommended optimal biological dose (OBD). Simulation studies showed that the proposed design shortened phase I trial duration relative to the comparator designs while maintaining competitive OBD-selection performance and acceptable safety. The seamless phase II component further reduced the need for additional enrollment and supported efficient final OBD selection.

</details>


### [106] [Merging Modal Clusters via Significance Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01958)
*Yong Wang,Shengwei Hu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Morse函数新性质的模态聚类合并方法，通过Kullback-Leibler散度评估聚类显著性，并按序合并最不显著聚类，有效减少聚类数量且避免密度失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决实际中常见的多余聚类问题并减少聚类数量，需要一种能保持密度结构且可解释性强的聚类合并策略。

Method: 利用Morse函数的新性质，定义聚类邻接关系，通过Kullback-Leibler散度或其对数似然近似评估各聚类的相对显著性，截断密度后逐个合并最不显著聚类。

Result: 该方法在数值实验中表现出色，能有效处理复杂聚类问题，相比现有主流方法产生更直观、更精确的聚类结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的模态聚类合并方法能够合理确定聚类数目，生成的层次聚类树具有良好的可解释性和实用性，适用于不同用户需求。

Abstract: To deal with superfluous clusters and to reduce the number of clusters as often desired in practice, a modal cluster merging procedure is proposed. Based on some new properties established in this paper for Morse functions, the procedure merges clusters in a sequential manner without causing unnecessary density distortion. Each cluster is evaluated for its significance relative to the other clusters, using the Kullback-Leibler divergence or its log-likelihood approximation, by truncating the density for the cluster at an appropriate level. The least significant cluster is then merged into one of its adjacent clusters, using the novel concept of cluster adjacency defined in this paper. The resulting hierarchical clustering tree is useful for determining the number of clusters, as may be preferred by a specific user or in a general, meaningful manner. Numerical studies show that the new procedure deals well with difficult clustering problems and often produces intuitively appealing and numerically more accurate clustering results, as compared with several other popular clustering methods in the literature.

</details>


### [107] [Testing for trends in online experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01973)
*Chris Haulk,Lee Richardson,Jacopo Soriano*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 使用线性回归结合刀切法置信区间可有效估计时变处理效应，在大型在线视频平台实验中表现接近现代单调趋势检验的功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索适用于怀疑存在趋势的在线实验中的时变处理效应估计方法。

Method: 采用线性回归与刀切法置信区间相结合的方法来估计时间变化的处理效果。

Result: 该方法在实际实验中表现出较高的统计功效，与现代单调趋势检验相比差距不大。

Conclusion: 若在线实验中可能存在趋势，建议考虑使用该估计方法。

Abstract: Time-varying treatment effects can be estimated with linear regression paired with jackknife confidence intervals. In experiments at a large online video platform, the test based on this estimator is not much less powerful than modern tests for monotonic trends. If you suspect trends in your online experiments, consider using this estimator.

</details>


### [108] [COSTA: Covariance-Optimized Design and Causal Inference under Network-Temporal Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02032)
*Qianyi Chen,Bo Li,Yongli Qin,Jinyong Ma*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了COSTA（协方差优化时空处理分配）方法，用于在具有网络溢出、时间延续性和依赖性的时序网络实验中优化处理分配。通过联合伯努利设计和阈值化高斯Kronecker参数化，实现了对均方误差的直接优化，并发展了针对处理依赖与干扰引起结果依赖的推断理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随时间观察的网络实验中，存在网络溢出效应、时间上的延续性以及由设计引入的依赖关系，传统方法难以有效处理这些复杂依赖，因此需要一种新的设计方法来减少估计偏差并提高估计效率。

Method: 提出COSTA——一种针对单元-时间点的联合伯努利处理分配设计；利用协方差层级的方差包络构建MSE界，并通过阈值化高斯Kronecker结构参数化实现可扩展性；发展基于潜变量块间典型相关的推断理论，结合图ψ中心极限定理和网络HAC理论进行统计推断。

Result: 在RetailRocket和MovieLens的半合成实验中，COSTA在多种设定下显著降低了RMSE，并展现出良好的区间校准性能，适用于线性、非线性和需求替代等多种结果表面。

Conclusion: COSTA提供了一种可扩展且高效的方法来应对网络实验中的时空依赖问题，能够在保证处理边际的同时优化协方差结构，从而提升因果效应估计的精度和推断的可靠性。

Abstract: Experiments on networks observed over time face network spillovers, temporal carryover, and dependence deliberately introduced by the design. We propose COSTA---Covariance-Optimized Spatiotemporal Treatment Allocation---a joint Bernoulli design for unit--time assignments. Under common treatment marginals and a nonnegative linear network--temporal exposure model, Horvitz--Thompson bias for the sustained all-treated versus all-control contrast is exactly the negative expected weight of an assignment cut. A covariance-level variance envelope yields an MSE bound that can be optimized directly over assignment covariance. To scale this design, we introduce a thresholded-Gaussian Kronecker parameterization that mirrors the network and temporal exposure operators while preserving valid Bernoulli marginals. We next develop inference theory for the joint effects of designed treatment dependence and interference-induced outcome dependence. Canonical correlations between latent blocks generating separated HT contributions supply the coefficients required by graph-$ψ$ central limit and network-HAC theory; a spectral-floor and far-row-mass condition gives a primitive sufficient check. The framework covers sparse, block, Kronecker, locally factored, and other structured covariance sequences satisfying these conditions. Semi-synthetic RetailRocket and MovieLens experiments show substantial default-setting RMSE reductions and well-calibrated model-assisted design-centered intervals across linear, nonlinear, and demand-substitution outcome surfaces.

</details>


### [109] [Data-Adaptive Rerandomization for 2K Factorial Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02078)
*Tingxuan Han,Ke Deng*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据自适应的重随机化框架，用于2^K因子设计，通过结合研究者对因子效应的优先级和效应特异性协变量重要性，提升估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重随机化方法未能充分利用因子效应间的异质性优先级和效应特异性协变量的重要性，导致协变量不平衡影响估计精度。

Method: 开发了一个联合考虑研究者优先级和协变量重要性的oracle准则，并提出数据自适应程序，从部分样本中学习效应特异性协变量重要性，应用于剩余样本。

Result: 在有限总体框架下建立了设计驱动的渐近理论，证明该方法保持了oracle设计的精度优先特性，并在适当条件下达到相同的渐近精度。数值研究表明其比现有方法有显著效率提升。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效提升了2^K因子设计中多主效应与交互效应估计的精度，兼具理论保证与实际可行性。

Abstract: Factorial designs allow simultaneous estimation of multiple main effects and interactions, but covariate imbalance can substantially reduce estimation precision. Existing rerandomization methods improve covariate balance yet do not fully exploit heterogeneous priorities across factorial effects or effect-specific covariate importance. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a data-adaptive rerandomization framework for $2^K$ factorial designs. We first develop an oracle criterion that jointly incorporates researchers' priorities over factorial effects and effect-specific covariate importance, enabling precision gains with guaranteed lower bounds. To make the oracle criterion implementable, we develop a data-adaptive procedure that learns effect-specific covariate importance from a random subset of units and applies an estimated oracle criterion to the remaining units. Unlike existing two-stage rerandomization methods for treatment-control experiments, our procedure accommodates multiple factorial effects and requires no auxiliary dataset. Under a finite-population framework, we establish design-based asymptotic theory and show that the proposed procedure preserves the oracle design's precision-prioritization property and, under suitable conditions, achieves the same asymptotic precision as the oracle design. Numerical studies demonstrate substantial efficiency gains over existing rerandomization methods.

</details>


### [110] [Efficient Screening Designs for Expensive Black-box Models with Qualitative and Quantitative Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02087)
*Difan Song,V. Roshan Joseph*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的最大单因素一次（MOFAT）设计方法，用于处理包含名义型、序数型和离散数值型等多种类型因素的黑箱模型筛选实验设计，并通过数值实验和机器学习超参数调优应用验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑箱模型的筛选设计主要关注连续型因素，难以有效处理多种类型输入因素并存的情况，限制了复杂模型优化和训练的效率。

Method: 识别最优筛选设计的性质，区分处理定性和定量因素，基于这些性质提出高效的算法来构建适用于各种类型因素的MOFAT设计。

Result: 所提出的MOFAT设计在数值实验中表现出良好的筛选性能，并成功应用于机器学习模型的超参数调优，提高了优化效率。

Conclusion: 扩展的MOFAT设计能够有效处理多类型输入因素，在复杂黑箱模型的因子筛选中具有实用价值和广泛适用性。

Abstract: Computationally expensive black-box models often involve a large number of input factors with complex interactions and varying importance. Experimental design techniques can be used for quickly identifying the important factors, which can make the optimization of a complex computer model or the training of an expensive machine learning model more efficient. Existing screening designs for black-box models focus mainly on continuous factors, with the maximum one-factor-at-a-time (MOFAT) design being a recent example. In this work, we extend the design to incorporate multiple types of factors, including nominal, ordinal, and discrete-numeric. We first identify the properties leading to optimal screening, where qualitative and quantitative factors should be treated differently. Based on these properties, we propose practical algorithms to efficiently construct MOFAT designs for all types of factors. The usefulness of the design is demonstrated by both numerical experiments and an application to hyperparameter tuning in machine learning models.

</details>


### [111] [Detecting Early and Late Divergences in Survival Curves Using Nonparametric Effect Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02596)
*Patrick B. Langthaler,Jun Ma,Jonas Beck*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对删失生存数据的非参数联合推断框架，用于检测治疗效果的早期与晚期差异，相比传统方法在非比例风险下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验中常见的治疗曲线早期发散或后期收敛等模式难以被传统的比例风险假设捕捉，因此需要一种能够同时评估全局和时间动态差异的新方法。

Method: 基于Kaplan-Meier估计的Mann-Whitney效应与一个新的时间对比指标，构建了适用于右删失数据的联合推断框架，并通过模拟研究验证其性能。

Result: 该方法在非比例风险情形下优于log-rank检验，能保持标称I类错误率，并揭示出标准分析中隐藏的早期治疗优势。

Conclusion: 所提框架提供了可解释的概率尺度效应度量，能有效识别生存曲线的时变差异，具有临床实用价值。

Abstract: Clinical trials often show treatment curves that diverge early and converge later, or vice versa patterns that are poorly captured by the proportional-hazards assumption. We develop a joint inferential framework for two nonparametric functionals of censored survival data: the Kaplan--Meier-based Mann--Whitney effect and a novel temporal contrast separating early and late differences. The approach provides interpretable, probability-scale effect measures and enables joint inference for global and temporal contrasts under right censoring. In simulation studies, the method outperforms the log-rank test under non-proportional hazards while maintaining nominal type-I error. A real-world application illustrates how the temporal contrast reveals clinically meaningful early treatment advantages that remain hidden in standard analyses

</details>


### [112] [Reconciling Interpretability with Covariate-Dependent Shape Flexibility in Penalized Transformation Models for Distributional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02662)
*Johannes Brachem,Thomas Kneib*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的惩罚变换模型（conditional-shape PTM），能够灵活建模响应变量条件分布的形状随协变量变化，同时保持对均值和标准差的直接解释性。该模型通过分离的结构化加性预测器分别建模条件均值、标准差和标准化分布形状，并利用单调变换和仿射标准化保证可解释性。采用小批量随机变分推断进行拟合，在模拟和实际数据中表现出良好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布回归中，如何在保持对均值和标准差直接解释的同时，灵活地让响应变量的条件分布形状随协变量变化，是一个核心挑战。现有方法往往难以兼顾灵活性与可解释性。

Method: 扩展了惩罚变换模型（PTM）家族，提出conditional-shape PTM，引入三个独立的结构化加性预测器分别建模条件均值、标准差和标准化后的分布形状；使用协变量依赖的单调变换将响应变量映射到固定参考分布，并通过仿射标准化确保标准化后分布的均值为0、方差为1；采用小批量随机变分推断与阶段性优化进行模型拟合。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该模型能准确恢复均值、标准差效应以及从偏态到双峰的形状变化，同时抑制不必要的形状效应；在误设场景下，其在测试集密度和分布函数预测精度上与结构化加性Dirichlet过程混合模型相当；在挪威水导电性和德国日气温的大样本数据中，揭示了选择性的群组、季节和空间相关的形状变异，且预测表现优于固定形状PTM和高斯位置-尺度模型。

Conclusion: conditional-shape PTM成功实现了在保留均值和标准差可解释性的同时，灵活建模条件分布形状的变化，是一种具有良好可解释性与预测性能的新型分布回归框架。

Abstract: A central challenge in distributional regression is to allow the shape of the conditional distribution of the response variable to vary flexibly with covariates while retaining directly interpretable effects on its mean and standard deviation. We extend the penalized transformation model (PTM) family into a conditional-shape PTM, which assigns separate structured additive predictors to the conditional mean, standard deviation, and standardized distributional shape beyond location and scale. A covariate-dependent monotone transformation maps the standardized response to a fixed reference distribution, while affine standardization enforces mean zero and variance one for the induced standardized distribution. Thus, the first two predictors remain exactly the conditional mean and standard deviation. The shape predictor accommodates selected linear, nonlinear, group-specific, spatial, and interaction effects; regularization shrinks unsupported departures toward a reference-family location-scale model. We fit the PTM using mini-batched stochastic variational inference with model-aligned Gaussian blocks and staged optimization. In simulations, the PTM recovers smooth mean and standard-deviation effects and a covariate-dependent transition from skewness to bimodality while suppressing unnecessary shape effects. Under a deliberately misspecified design, it remains competitive with a structured additive Dirichlet-process mixture in test-set density and distribution-function accuracy, although all models show undercoverage and both flexible methods miss fine features. Applications to $13{,}425$ Norwegian water-conductivity observations and $1{,}182{,}514$ German daily-temperature observations demonstrate selective group-specific, seasonal, and spatial shape variation. Predictive performance criteria favor the conditional-shape PTM over a fixed-shape PTM and a Gaussian location-scale model.

</details>


### [113] [Off-policy causal estimation in networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02756)
*Sahil Loomba,Dean Eckles*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了在存在干扰的情况下，基于异质伯努利策略的因果效应的离策略估计问题，提出了一种基于偏置傅里叶基的暴露加权潜在结果表示方法，并构建了最小L²权重以实现子空间内的策略转移，同时分析了偏差-方差权衡与鲁棒性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在干扰的环境中，传统因果估计依赖于特定处理分配策略，难以推广到其他干预策略，因此需要发展适用于不同策略下的离策略因果推断方法。

Method: 通过将暴露加权的潜在结果表示在实验设计的偏置傅里叶基上，针对预设的傅里叶子空间（编码假设的干扰结构），构造唯一的最小L²权重以实现函数在该子空间上的策略迁移，并推导方差的可识别界与保守估计量。

Result: 提出了统一框架，涵盖全局/局部逆概率权重等多种现有方法作为特例；给出了结构化卡方距离形式的权重方差表达式、偏差-方差权衡关系、傅里叶邻域重叠条件下的相合性，以及Doob鞅中心极限定理和方差的保守估计。

Conclusion: 该方法为在网络干扰和设计不匹配情境下进行实验设计与分析提供了理论支持，能够在干扰结构被误设时提供清晰的鲁棒性边界，并实现有效的离策略因果效应估计。

Abstract: In the presence of interference, where the treatment assigned to one unit can affect the outcomes of others, many causal estimands depend on the treatment-assignment policy under which the experiment is conducted. This policy dependence creates a fundamental challenge for off-policy estimation, where the goal is to estimate causal quantities under a hypothetical intervention policy different from the one used to collect data. We study this problem of off-policy estimation of causal effects for heterogeneous Bernoulli policies. By representing exposure-weighted potential outcomes in the biased Fourier basis of the experimental design, we construct, for any prespecified Fourier subspace encoding the assumed interference structure, the unique minimum-$L^2$ weight that transports every function in that subspace. Global and local inverse-probability weights, linear-interference weights, and no-interference weights are special cases. The weight variance is a structured chi-square distance between the experiment and target policies. When the assumed interference structure is misspecified, the introduced bias couples the omitted outcome spectrum with the corresponding policy-shift coefficients, yielding a sharp robustness bound and a bias-variance trade-off. A Fourier-neighborhood-overlap condition gives consistency under structured interference, and we state a Doob-martingale central limit theorem for off-policy estimators. As the variance is not identified, we derive identifiable bounds and associated conservative estimators of the variance. Simulations illustrate these theoretical results for the design and analysis of experiments under network interference and design mismatch.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum mutual information statistics for detecting dependence-structure change points in time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02787)
*Jiwon Kang,Yun Am Seo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子互信息（QMI）的新型统计方法，用于检测多元时间序列中两个分量间依赖关系的变化，能够在无需密度估计或调参的情况下有效区分纯依赖变化与边缘漂移，具有更高的检测效能和更低的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以在边缘分布自由漂移的情况下准确检测变量间依赖结构的变化，因此需要一种对依赖关系敏感且鲁棒的统计量。

Method: 将单位范数随机傅里叶特征的秩的二阶矩归一化为密度算子，利用其部分迹恢复边际算子，并通过冯·诺依曼熵计算量子互信息（QMI）作为统计量；结合惩罚性最优划分、Holevo信息增益、联合对置换校准及可交换性诊断实现推断。

Result: QMI在500次重复实验中比HSIC、距离相关、Spearman和经验Copula等方法至少高出15个百分点的检测能力，且在边缘漂移下误报率接近标称水平；在韩国八年气象数据中，两阶段‘分割-验证’流程成功识别出纯耦合变化并有效剔除由边缘驱动的假阳性。

Conclusion: QMI是一种无需密度估计、无需调参、计算高效的依赖变化检测方法，在理论和实践中均表现出优越性能，尤其适用于存在边缘漂移的实际时间序列分析场景。

Abstract: Detecting when the dependence between two components of a multivariate time series changes, while the marginals drift freely, requires a dependence-specific statistic. We take the inferential object to be a density operator -- the trace-normalised second moment of unit-norm random Fourier features of ranks -- rather than a probability distribution. Partial traces recover the marginal operators exactly, so von Neumann entropies yield a quantum mutual information (QMI) statistic computed from prefix sums of small matrices, without density estimation, matrix inversion, or a tuned parameter. We develop the inference it needs: a segment-separable cost that drives penalised optimal partitioning, its split gain a Holevo information; finite-sample exact calibration by joint pair permutation, a block-permutation form for serially dependent series, and an exact, provably consistent exchangeability diagnostic that selects between them. We also prove a weighted chi-square boundary law, at the segment length and not its square root, for the rank-based statistic exactly as computed. In 500 replicates QMI detects nonlinear, correlation-free dependence changes with more power than the Hilbert-Schmidt independence criterion, distance correlation, Spearman, and empirical-copula statistics on the same ranks, by at least 15 percentage points wherever any statistic detects the change. Its false-alarm rate stays near nominal under marginal drift, where the empirical-copula statistic reaches 0.87. On eight years of hourly Korean weather observations, a two-stage segment-and-certify procedure finds dependence-change candidates above chance (five of 27 at p $\le$ 0.05 against 1.4 expected); stage two certifies one as a pure coupling change and reclassifies eight as marginal-driven.

</details>


### [115] [Non-Invariance in Nested Prediction Models under Selective Predictor Availability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02836)
*Marc Delord*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种嵌套预测模型框架，用于分析健康数据中选择性测量预测因子对临床预测模型开发和验证的影响，揭示了因变量缺失、残差非不变性及其交互作用导致的模型偏差，并指出在选定人群中基于条件分布进行插补会引入插补偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于常规健康数据中常存在预测因子的选择性测量问题，导致模型在不同人群间可能缺乏普适性，本文旨在建立一个理论框架来系统刻画这种选择性测量带来的影响。

Method: 采用嵌套预测模型的方法，定义目标人群中的限制模型和包含选择性测量预测因子的扩展模型，分析模型在不同人群间的非不变性分解成分，并扩展至多路径选择机制及碰撞结构情形，同时研究基于选定人群条件分布的插补方法所引入的偏倚。

Result: 发现限制模型在选定患者与目标人群之间的非不变性可分解为三个部分：额外预测因子的遗漏、潜在的残差非不变性及其交互作用；当使用选择性测量路径时，基于选定人群进行插补会将限制模型的非不变性传递到扩展模型中，形成插补偏倚；该框架适用于如尿白蛋白/肌酐比值这类临床实践中选择性测量的指标。

Conclusion: 选择性测量预测因子会导致临床预测模型出现偏差，特别是在模型外推和插补过程中可能引入系统性误差，因此在利用常规收集的健康数据构建或验证模型时，必须谨慎考虑测量选择机制的影响。

Abstract: Selective measurement of predictors is common in routinely collected health data. We used nested prediction models as a framework for characterising the consequences of a selectively measured predictor, with a restricted model defined in the target population and an extended model including the selectively measured predictor defined in a selected population. We show that non-invariance in the restricted model between selected patients and the target population decomposes into components due to omission of the additional predictor, potential residual non-invariance, and their interaction. We extended this framework to predictors measured through multiple routes of selection resulting in collider structures. We further show that imputation based on the conditional distribution of the additional predictor in the selected population transfers restricted-model non-invariance to the imputed extended model as imputation bias. We illustrate the framework using the Kidney Failure Risk Equation, where albumin-to-creatinine ratio is selectively measured in routine clinical practice. The framework provides a formal basis for understanding how selective predictor measurement may affect the development and validation of clinical prediction models using routinely collected health data.

</details>
