### [116] [Learning a general class of admissible multi-species collision operators from molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01845)
*Yue Zhao,Andrew Christlieb,Huan Lei*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构保持的数据驱动碰撞算子，用于多组分动力学系统，能够从分子动力学数据中学习并准确预测输运系数和非平衡弛豫过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更准确地描述多组分体系中由微观粒子相互作用引起的非平衡动量和能量交换，特别是传统Landau算子无法准确刻画的中等耦合等离子体动力学。

Method: 基于局部可识别的核函数类，构建满足守恒律、H定理和框架无关性的碰撞算子；引入非对称交叉项核以反映不同物种间的非对称响应，并通过低秩张量表示和随机采样实现高效学习与模拟。

Result: 所学习的算子能精确预测输运系数和非平衡弛豫行为，保持离散守恒和熵增特性，并在中等耦合等离子体 regime 中表现出优于Landau及对称性限制模型的性能。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动碰撞算子通过保留关键物理结构和引入结构自由度，成功捕捉了多组分系统中的复杂多体效应，为从分子动力学构建宏观动力学模型提供了新途径。

Abstract: We develop a structure-preserving, data-driven collision operator for spatially homogeneous multi-species kinetic systems from molecular dynamics (MD). The operator consists of diagonal self-collision blocks and ordered off-diagonal cross-species blocks to describe intra- and inter-species momentum and energy exchange. Within a local and point-wise identifiable kernel class, we develop the necessary and sufficient condition for the admissible kernel class satisfying the conservation laws, the H-theorem, and the frame indifference. Unlike the classical Landau operator, the off-diagonal kernels are not restricted to be symmetric under permutation of the two velocity variables. This unique structural freedom captures the distinct responses of different species to unresolved correlations and many-body effects arising from micro-scale particle interactions. The equivalent parameterizable kernel formalization enables us to learn a generalized data-driven collision operator directly from MD, where the low-rank tensor representations and random sampling are used to achieve efficient kernel training and numerical simulation. Numerical experiments show that the learned operator accurately predicts transport coefficients and the non-equilibrium relaxation, while retaining discrete conservation and entropy production. In particular, it captures plasma kinetics in the moderately coupled regime, where the predictions of both the Landau and the data-driven model restricted to velocity-permutation symmetry show significant discrepancies.

</details>


### [117] [Physics-informed Learning for Orbital Uncertainty Propagation with Error Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01881)
*Chun-Wei Kong,Morteza Lahijanian,Jay McMahon*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）的方法，结合高斯混合模型（PINN-GMM）和误差学习机制，用于求解轨道动力学中的Fokker-Planck方程，实现高维非线性系统下的不确定性传播与误差边界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于轨道动力学中存在非线性运动、高维状态和大时空域，传统方法难以有效求解Fokker-Planck方程并准确传播不确定性，因此需要一种坐标系无关且能提供误差界的方法。

Method: 设计了物理信息高斯混合模型（PINN-GMM）以满足概率密度函数的性质，并引入辅助的误差PINN来学习近似误差的动力学行为，生成时间依赖的误差上下界，构成PDF的模糊集，从而通过线性规划计算事件概率的上下界。

Result: 在1D示例及4D–6D轨道测试案例中，该方法实现了精确的不确定性传播，提供了正确且具信息性的误差边界，在可靠性上优于高斯近似、无迹变换和标准高斯混合模型等基线方法；单次前向推理可在0.16毫秒内完成密度预测。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够高效、准确地进行高维非线性系统中的不确定性传播，并提供严格的误差界限，提升了轨道预测的可靠性，尽管训练阶段成本较高，但推理速度快，适用于实时应用。

Abstract: The Fokker-Planck partial differential equation (FP-PDE) governs uncertainty evolution in stochastic dynamical systems. In orbital dynamics, solving the FP-PDE is challenging because of nonlinear motion, high-dimensional states, and large space-time domains. We develop a physics-informed neural network (PINN) approach that approximates the FP-PDE solution as a single space-time probability density, while also quantifying its worst-case approximation error. This approach is, in principle, independent of the choice of state coordinates and neural network architecture. Specifically, to enforce probability density function (PDF) properties into the neural network, we design a Physics-informed Gaussian mixture model (PINN-GMM). Then a companion error PINN learns the dynamics of the approximation error and yields time-dependent bounds that define an ambiguity set of PDFs. This ambiguity set enables rigorous computation of upper and lower bounds on event probabilities through tractable linear programs. Numerical studies on illustrative 1D examples and several 4D--6D orbital test cases demonstrate accurate uncertainty propagation, correct and informative error bounds, and improved reliability over common uncertainty-propagation baseline methods (Gaussian approximation, unscented transform, and Gaussian mixture model). Constructing the PINN-GMM requires offline training, making it costlier than the baseline approximations; once trained, however, a single forward pass returns the density at any time in sub-millisecond time $(0.16~\mathrm{ms}$ in our implementation).

</details>


### [118] [RBMD 2.0: Random batch molecular dynamics package for large-scale simulations on multi-GPU architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02694)
*Qi Zhou,Yongfa Guo,Jincheng Zhong,Shou-Hang Bo,Teng Zhao,Zhenli Xu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: RBMD 2.0是一个面向多GPU架构的大规模分子动力学模拟软件新版本，通过改进的随机批次Ewald方法和三维域分解技术，显著加速了非键合力计算，具有高弱扩展性和跨平台可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分子动力学模拟在多GPU架构上常受限于非键合力计算的计算与通信开销，亟需高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 结合改进的随机批次Ewald方法、三维域分解和幽灵粒子通信，并基于DTK CUDA框架实现跨异构加速器架构的可移植性。

Result: 在多达上亿粒子的多设备模拟中，非键合力计算速度提升数倍至约两个数量级，弱扩展性超过97.5%。

Conclusion: RBMD 2.0展现出作为未来百亿亿次分子动力学模拟计算引擎的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Large-scale molecular dynamics simulations of particle systems on multi-GPU architectures are often constrained by the computational and communication costs of nonbonded force evaluation. We present RBMD 2.0, a major new release of the random batch molecular dynamics package designed for cross-node multi-GPU simulations of large-scale systems. It combines the improved random batch Ewald method with three-dimensional domain decomposition and ghost-particle communication to accelerate multi-GPU nonbonded force evaluation, while the DTK CUDA framework facilitates portability across heterogeneous accelerator architectures. Numerical experiments on multiple benchmark systems demonstrate both the accuracy and efficiency of simulations with RBMD 2.0. For simulations involving up to hundreds of millions of particles across multiple accelerator devices, one achieves speedups ranging from severalfold to approximately two orders of magnitude in nonbonded force evaluation while exhibiting over $97.5\%$ weak-scaling behavior. These results demonstrate the promising nature of RBMD 2.0 as a computational engine for future exascale molecular dynamics simulations.

</details>


### [119] [Learning Spectral-Like Mesh-Free Discretisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02833)
*Lucas Gerken Starepravo,Henry Broadley,Steven Lind,Jack R. C. King*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Spectral-like Neural Discretisation (SpeND)的新方法，通过将局部模板中自由度的选择转化为神经网络学习问题，提升无网格微分算子在高频波数下的精度，同时严格保证多项式一致性，并在无监督、不依赖物理先验的情况下显著扩展了有效频带范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无网格方法在构造微分算子时仅通过多项式一致性约束低波数行为，对包含精细结构的高波数区域缺乏控制，导致高频误差较大；且欠定系统中多余自由度的隐式选择未针对频域性能优化。本文旨在显式优化全频带逼近性能，尤其是提高可解析波数范围内的精度。

Method: 提出SpeND方法，使用条件神经网络参数化 stencil 权重，网络输入为局部节点几何，输出经硬约束投影层映射到满足多项式一致性的仿射子空间；训练目标为最小化预设频带函数空间上的模态响应误差（色散与耗散），采用自监督、物理无关的方式进行学习。

Result: 在二维无序节点分布上的模态分析表明，所学的四阶算子在可解析频带内的响应远优于相同模板大小的LABFM及结构化网格上的四阶有限差分法，有效频带显著拓宽，同时保持了预期的四阶收敛率。

Conclusion: SpeND通过神经网络学习与硬约束一致性结合，实现了高精度、高收敛性的无网格微分算子构造，为无网格方法在复杂几何和高分辨率模拟中的应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Meshfree methods such as smoothed particle hydrodynamics (SPH) with kernel corrections, radial basis function-generated finite differences (RBF-FD), and the local anisotropic basis function method (LABFM) construct discrete differential operators by imposing polynomial consistency on a local stencil. For stencils containing more nodes than there are consistency constraints, the resulting linear system is underdetermined, and the remaining degrees of freedom are fixed implicitly by the choice of kernel, basis preconditioning, or a minimum-norm condition. Polynomial consistency constrains the operator only in the low-wavenumber limit, and no part of the construction selects for accuracy at the wavenumbers where fine-scale content resides. We introduce Spectral-like Neural Discretisation (SpeND), in which the choice of those degrees of freedom is cast as a learning problem: stencil weights are parametrised by a neural network conditioned on the local node geometry, trained to approximate the modal response of a spectral operator over the resolvable band. A hard-constrained projection layer maps the network output onto the affine subspace of consistent weights, so that polynomial consistency holds exactly by construction rather than as a penalty. Training is self-supervised and physics-agnostic, requiring no reference solutions; the objective minimises dispersion and dissipation error over a prescribed band-limited function space. Modal analysis on disordered two-dimensional node distributions shows that the learned fourth-order operator follows the exact response over a substantially wider band than either explicit LABFM at equal stencil size or fourth-order finite differences on a structured grid, whilst recovering the expected fourth-order convergence rate under refinement.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [A Control Co-Design Framework to Optimize Sustainability with Application to Microgrid-Driven Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01912)
*Tania Rifat Jahan,Donald J. Docimo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以可持续性为核心的控制协同设计（CCD）框架，用于优化能源系统的设备特性与控制器参数，特别应用于微电网驱动的数据中心，结果表明该框架能显著提升环境可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在数据中⼼和微⽹设计中缺乏对设备与控制器的联合优化，且忽视了可持续性指标；同时，数据中心和支撑其运行的电网技术消耗大量能源，可能加剧温室气体排放和电子废弃物问题，亟需兼顾全生命周期的可持续性优化方法。

Method: 建立一个通用的、以可持续性为中心的控制协同设计（CCD）框架，将可持续性指标分为制造、运行和处置三个生命周期阶段，并将其应用于微电网驱动的数据中心系统，实现设备与控制器参数的联合优化。

Result: 框架成功生成了一系列与设备和控制器参数相关的可持续性指标，揭示了组件制造阶段的温室气体排放可能远超运行阶段；相比传统设计方法，新框架下的系统设计在多个环境可持续性方面有显著改善。

Conclusion: 所提出的可持续性导向的控制协同设计框架有效填补了当前在能源系统设计中缺乏联合优化与全生命周期可持续性考量的空白，为数据中⼼和微⽹等能源系统的绿色设计提供了有力工具。

Abstract: This work studies the optimization of physical plant characteristics and controller parameters for sustainability. Environmental sustainability is strongly correlated with the development and operation of energy systems, with data centers as the preeminent modern example. Data centers, and the grid technologies that provide their power, consume nonnegligible amounts of global energy and pose a risk to increase greenhouse gas (GHG) emissions and electronic waste. Addressing such issues requires improved plant design and control strategies, often approached through optimization-based methods. However, there are noticeable gaps regarding data center and microgrid design: (i) simultaneous optimization of plant and controller features is rarely explored, and (ii) sustainability criteria are not emphasized. This work addresses these gaps by establishing a generalized, sustainability-centric control co-design (CCD) framework for energy systems. Uniquely, the CCD framework defines and categorizes sustainability metrics into three lifecycle stages - manufacturing, operation, and disposal - supporting optimization and comparative analysis of the metrics for different design options. To exemplify its use, the CCD framework is applied to a microgrid-driven data center system, providing a family of sustainability metrics correlated to plant and controller parameters. The analysis enabled by the framework provides insights into the CCD of microgrids and data centers, such as that GHG-equivalent emissions from manufacturing of components can dwarf those generated during operation of the system. The system designs identified by the proposed framework show substantial improvements to environmental sustainability categories as compared to designs identified through baseline procedures.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum-Based k-Coverage Optimization for UAV-Aided Search and Rescue Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01930)
*Halim Lee,Suhui Jeong,Na Young Kim,Jiwon Seo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子计算的无人机搜救路径规划方法，通过构建精确惩罚的QUBO模型并应用于QAOA算法，在模拟器和IBM量子硬件上实现了高效的射频信号覆盖定位，实验结果表明该方法在覆盖率和路径优化方面优于传统基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模灾害中，快速定位失踪人员是搜救行动的关键挑战。利用无人机接收移动设备发出的射频信号进行定位具有潜力，但如何高效规划无人机航点以实现充分覆盖仍是一个难题。

Method: 将无人机航点选择问题建模为扩展的k-覆盖问题，并定义了独立的无人机可飞行区域与目标区域；构建了带有充分惩罚条件的精确惩罚QUBO模型，并转换为伊辛代价哈密顿量，使用QAOA算法在噪声模拟器和IBM 127比特Eagle处理器上进行求解。

Result: 在模拟器测试中，QAOA成功恢复已知最小航点解；在硬件测试中，矩形区域的平均三重覆盖率超过95%，校园规模测试中十次运行达到99.3%平均覆盖率且90%运行可行，最优可行路径比传统网格方法缩短最多37.0%。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了用于射频搜救航点选择的精确QUBO表示形式，并验证了其在当前门级量子硬件上的可行性与优势，展示了量子优化在实际搜救任务中的应用前景。

Abstract: In large-scale disaster scenarios, rapid localization of missing persons is a critical challenge for search-and-rescue (SAR) operations. Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) equipped with radio frequency (RF) receivers can support RF-based localization by collecting signals emitted from mobile devices at spatially distributed sensing locations. This paper addresses the resulting waypoint-selection problem: determining a minimum set of UAV waypoints that provides at least threefold coverage of every potential target location. We formulate this task as an extended k-coverage problem that independently defines the UAV-navigable and target regions, and derive an exact-penalty quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) formulation with a sufficient penalty condition that preserves feasibility and minimum waypoint cardinality. The QUBO is mapped to an Ising-form cost Hamiltonian and evaluated using the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) on both a noise-free simulator and IBM's 127-qubit Eagle processor. On the tested simulator instances, QAOA recovers the known minimum-cardinality solutions. Across the rectangular hardware test cases, the mean 3-coverage ratio exceeded 95%. In the campus-scale evaluation, ten hardware executions achieved 99.3% mean 3-coverage with a 90% feasible-run rate, while the shortest feasible flight path was up to 37.0% shorter than those of the deterministic grid-based baselines. Additional comparisons with classical optimization and learning-based baselines are provided, together with computational and quantum-resource analyses for larger generated instances. These results establish an exact QUBO representation for RF-based SAR waypoint selection and characterize its implementation on current gate-based quantum hardware.

</details>


### [122] [Dimension-Reduced ADP for Real-Time Microgrid Operation with Massive Air-Conditioning Loads under Multiple Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01972)
*Jingguan Liu,Xiaomeng Ai,Shichang Cui,Jiakun Fang,Jinyu Wen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种降维近似动态规划方法，用于在多重不确定性下实时优化含大量空调负荷的微电网运行，具有低计算成本和良好可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模空调负荷和多重不确定性带来的微电网实时运行优化中的维度灾难问题。

Method: 将运行问题建模为多阶段马尔可夫决策过程，引入后决策价值函数，并采用基于一致性的价值函数投影将高维状态空间降维为聚合状态空间，结合分段线性近似进行高效价值函数训练。

Result: 在33节点和123节点系统上的案例研究表明，该方法在确定性和随机条件下均能实现接近最优的运行性能，且计算成本低、可扩展性好。

Conclusion: 所提出的降维ADP方法有效克服了高维状态空间的挑战，适用于大规模空调负荷参与的微电网实时优化调度。

Abstract: This paper proposes a dimension-reduced approximate dynamic programming (ADP) method for real-time microgrid operation with massive air-conditioning loads under multiple uncertainties. The operation problem is formulated as a multi-stage Markov decision process, and a post-decision value function is introduced to characterize the impact of current decisions on future operating costs. To address the curse of dimensionality caused by massive air-conditioning loads, a consistency-based value function projection is developed to map the high-dimensional state space at each node into a tractable aggregated state space. Based on the reduced states, piecewise linear approximation is further employed for efficient value function training. Case studies on 33-bus and 123-bus systems show that the proposed method achieves near-optimal operation performance with low computational cost and good scalability under both deterministic and stochastic conditions.

</details>


### [123] [Efficient Sensor Fusion Through Covariance-Constrained Observation Decimation (CCOD)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02010)
*Andres Enriquez Fernandez,Erik Blasch,Angel Flores-Abad,John J. Bird*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协方差约束观测降采样（CCOD）框架，通过重构DARE输入来直接预测降采样条件下的稳态估计误差协方差，无需增加系统维度或求解周期性Riccati方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在状态估计中，观测降采样可降低资源消耗，但会增加估计不确定性；需要准确预测降采样后的估计性能。

Method: 通过等效的降采样系统和过程噪声矩阵重构DARE输入，以捕捉测量更新间的协方差增长，并利用单次DARE求解实现稳态协方差预测。

Result: 在高维线性时不变系统和空间目标跟踪应用中验证了该方法能准确预测估计性能，并有效减少满足协方差约束所需的测量频率。

Conclusion: CCOD框架能够高效、准确地预测降采样下Kalman滤波器的稳态性能，为在保证估计精度的前提下优化采样频率提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Observation decimation is frequently employed in state estimation to reduce sensing, communication, and computational requirements, but decreasing the measurement assimilation frequency increases estimation uncertainty. Selecting an appropriate observation decimation factor therefore requires accurately predicting the resulting estimator performance. While the discrete algebraic Riccati equation (DARE) provides the steady-state estimation-error covariance for standard linear time-invariant Kalman filters, it is not directly applicable to estimators employing decimated measurement updates. Existing approaches address this limitation through lifted system representations or periodic Riccati equation formulations, both of which incur additional computational complexity. This paper presents a covariance-constrained observation decimation (CCOD) framework that reformulates the DARE inputs using equivalent decimated system and process-noise matrices that capture covariance growth between measurement updates. The proposed reformulation enables direct prediction of the steady-state estimation-error covariance through a single DARE evaluation without increasing the system dimension or solving coupled periodic Riccati equations. The resulting covariance prediction is used to determine the maximum observation decimation factor that satisfies a prescribed estimation uncertainty bound. Validation using a high dimensional linear time-invariant system and a space object tracking application demonstrates that the proposed approach accurately predicts steady-state estimator performance while reducing the measurement assimilation frequency required to satisfy specified covariance constraints.

</details>


### [124] [Seed-Anchored Budget-Bounded Graph Rendering for Question Answering on Industry-Standard Power-Grid Information and Exchange Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02011)
*Jayakumar Manoharan,Yamini Sehgal*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于种子锚定的图渲染方法，用于在固定上下文预算下提升电力网络模型上的问答性能，相比传统方法显著提高了多跳查询的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电力系统模型（如CIM/CGMES）中进行大语言模型问答时，受限于上下文长度，难以有效保留与问题相关的多跳图结构信息，因此需要一种无需额外学习且能确定性保留关键局部证据的渲染方法。

Method: 引入种子锚定图渲染（seed-anchored graph rendering），通过预定义的种子节点优先保留查询局部的图证据，在贪婪生成受限上下文前缀时保证答案相关单元不被丢弃，仅依赖共享的跳数限制和上下文预算，不引入额外可调参数。

Result: 在两个预算受限的CGMES编码上，传统方法对多跳项目仅保留0.12和0.00的局部证据，而种子锚定方法保留了全部；在SmallGrid拓扑族的100个新测试项上，准确率从0.450提升至0.970（8,000字符预算内）；在标准流程下，该方法优于LightRAG、GraphRAG等提取式图表示方法，并避免使用LLM生成图结构。

Conclusion: 种子锚定图渲染能在固定上下文预算下高效保留查询相关的多跳证据，显著提升电力网络模型上的问答准确率，且无需训练或额外消耗token构建图结构，适用于标准化工况下的模型交换场景。

Abstract: Large language model question answering over power-grid models must respect a fixed context budget. We introduce seed-anchored graph rendering, a deterministic method that prioritizes query-local graph evidence without adding method-specific tuned or learned parameters beyond the shared hop bound and context budget. The method provides a checkable condition under which predefined seed-local answer-bearing render units are preserved in a greedy bounded-context prefix. We evaluate the approach on Common Information Model (CIM) network models exchanged through the Common Grid Model Exchange Standard (CGMES). On two budget-binding CGMES encodings, naive descriptions-first rendering retains local evidence for every single-hop item but only 0.12 and 0.00 of multi-hop items, whereas seed-anchored rendering retains all such evidence. On a preregistered fresh 100-item bank from the SmallGrid topology family, accuracy rises from 0.450 to 0.970 under a fixed 8,000-character context budget. Under a common retrieval and rendering pipeline, the standards-native seed-anchored graph matches or exceeds extracted graph representations produced by LightRAG, Microsoft GraphRAG, and HippoRAG, while avoiding LLM graph-construction tokens. The results are specific to the evaluated CIM/CGMES models, reader, and context budget; they concern budget-bounded retrieval rather than general question answering.

</details>


### [125] [Turnpike properties in nonlinear system identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02071)
*Julian D. Schiller,Matthias A. Müller*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散时间非线性状态空间模型在模拟误差最小化（SEM）方法下的累积输出转轨特性，建立了该性质与价值函数强制性及严格耗散性之间的等价关系，并提出了较弱的基数转轨性质，给出了基于增量输出稳定性等条件的充分条件，通过数值例子验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在使用SEM方法学习非线性系统时，常固定初始状态以降低计算复杂度，但需确保其解接近无约束情况下的最优解。本文旨在为这种做法提供理论依据，特别是在黑箱建模中存在多个最优输出序列的情况下。

Method: 通过分析SEM优化问题中的累积输出转轨性质，结合可达性条件，建立其与价值函数的强制性和严格耗散性的等价关系；引入基数转轨概念并比较其强弱；基于增量输出稳定性、阶段成本凸性和最优性条件推导出转轨行为的充分条件。

Result: 证明了在温和可达条件下，累积转轨性质、价值函数强制性和特定形式的严格耗散性三者等价；提出了更弱的基数转轨性质；给出了保证转轨行为的充分条件，并通过数值算例验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 累积输出转轨性质为采用固定初态的简化SEM方法提供了理论支持，使其在实际应用中既能保持计算可行性，又能逼近全局最优性能。

Abstract: We analyze the problem of learning general discrete-time nonlinear state-space models using the simulation error minimization (SEM) method. In this setting, model parameters are typically learned by minimizing the mismatch between simulated and measured outputs over a training dataset, or shorter subsequences extracted from it. Specifically, we study the cumulative output turnpike property of the underlying SEM optimization problem, which requires optimal output sequences emanating from a fixed initial state to approach and remain close to an optimal output sequence of the corresponding SEM problem with free initial state. In the presence of non-unique optimal output sequences---as may arise, for instance, in fully black-box system identification using neural networks---the property is formulated with respect to the closest such sequence. Turnpike behavior is generally desirable in practice, as it provides a theoretical justification for employing computationally more tractable SEM formulations with fixed initial states while ensuring that their optimal output sequences remain close to unconstrained optimal ones. Under a mild reachability condition, we establish equivalence between the cumulative turnpike property, coercivity of the value function, and a tailored notion of strict dissipativity. We additionally introduce a cardinality turnpike property and show that it is strictly weaker than the cumulative notion. Finally, we establish sufficient conditions for turnpike behavior based on incremental output stability, convexity of the stage cost, and a suitable optimality condition, and illustrate the theory by means of a numerical example.

</details>


### [126] [Existential Opacity for Discrete-Event Systems with State Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02132)
*Zhiyuan Huang,Zhao Tong,Jiakai Li,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的状态观测下的离散事件系统存在性-opacity（existential opacity, EO）概念，相较于传统opacity更宽松且更具表达力，适用于路径规划等应用，并提供了相应的验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于状态观测的opacity概念无法充分描述系统中可被利用于保持-opacity的任务执行的秘密行为的存在性，因此需要一种更灵活的新概念。

Method: 提出了存在性-opacity（EO）的新定义，仅要求存在某些不可被外部入侵者区分的秘密行为，并发展了若干EO性质及其验证方法。

Result: 证明了EO比现有的基于状态观测的opacity更具表达能力，并给出了一系列可用于分析离散事件系统中EO的验证方法。

Conclusion: 存在性-opacity为-opacity保持任务提供了新的可行性判断标准，拓展了opacity在实际应用中的适用范围。

Abstract: Opacity is a fundamental system property for confidentiality in discrete-event systems (DES). Classical opacity is typically defined under event-based observations, requiring that any secret system behavior remains indistinguishable from some non-secret behavior to an external intruder. However, in many applications such as path planning or opacity-preserving tasks, the intruder observes system states rather than events. Moreover, it often suffices that the system exhibits secret behaviors that can be exploited for opacity-preserving task execution, but such a system property cannot be fully captured by existing notions of state-observation-based opacity. Motivated by this limitation, we propose a relaxed notion of existing state-observation-based opacity, called existential opacity (EO), which only requires the existence of secret behaviors (instead of all secret behaviors) that are indistinguishable from a non-secret behavior under the state observations of the intruder. We show that the notion of EO is more expressive than existing state-observation-based opacity notions. In addition, a class of EO properties together with their corresponding verification approaches are developed, enabling the analysis of existential opacity in discrete-event systems and providing a new criterion for determining the feasibility of opacity-preserving problems.

</details>


### [127] [Prosumer Synchronisation Risk: Impacts of Time-Varying Tariffs on Distribution Network Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02174)
*Dillon Zadoks,Alfredo Oneto,Carlo Tajoli,Yi Guo,Philipp Heer,Giovanni Sansavini,Gabriela Hug*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了分时电价对低压电网扩容需求的影响，发现虽然分时电价可显著降低电网投资成本，但当用户采用率超过一定阈值后，反而会因行为同步导致新的负荷高峰，增加扩容需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式能源的快速部署，传统固定电价已难以适应电网运行需求，配电系统运营商希望通过分时电价引导用户行为以延缓电网升级，但需评估其可能引发的用户同步响应风险。

Method: 采用混合整数线性模型，在瑞士471个低压参考网络上模拟2050年分布式能源发展情景，分析两种分时电价（分时电价和实时电价）下用户成本最小化行为对电网扩容需求的影响。

Result: 采用分时电价可使电网扩容投资成本降低50%以上，但当用户采用率超过临界阈值（分时电价50%，实时电价40%）后，再增加20%的采用率将导致扩容需求分别上升39%和134%。

Conclusion: 分时电价在适度采用时可有效延缓电网升级，但高采用率会引发用户行为同步，造成新的负荷峰值，反而加剧电网压力，因此需谨慎设计电价政策的推广范围与机制。

Abstract: The rapid deployment of distributed energy resources, including heat pumps, electric vehicles, photovoltaics, and battery storage, is reshaping the operation of low-voltage networks. While distribution system operators often aim to develop time-varying tariffs to incentivise network-friendly behaviour and defer reinforcements, they risk triggering prosumer synchronisation. As prosumers follow price signals, their behaviour may synchronise, avoiding existing load peaks while creating undesirable peaks at other times. This paper quantifies the impact on low-voltage network reinforcement needs when shifting from flat tariffs to two time-varying tariffs: Time-of-Use and Real-Time Pricing. Applying a mixed-integer linear model across 471 low-voltage reference networks in Switzerland, we evaluate expansion requirements under projections of distributed energy resource deployment for 2050. In the model, prosumers operate their distributed energy resources in accordance with their subscribed electricity tariff to minimise costs, and the distribution system operator subsequently optimises reinforcements. Our results demonstrate that while adopting time-varying tariffs can reduce reinforcement investment costs by more than 50%, high adoption rates can reverse this declining trend. Specifically, once prosumer adoption exceeds the optimal thresholds of 50% for Time-of-Use and 40% for Real-Time Pricing, reinforcement needs increase by 39% and 134%, respectively, for an additional 20% of tariff adoption.

</details>


### [128] [Continuous-Time Aggregation of Massive Flexible HVAC Loads Considering Uncertainty for Reserve Provision in Power System Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02408)
*Jingguan Liu,Xiaomeng Ai,Jiakun Fang,Shichang Cui,Shengshi Wang,Wei Yao,Jinyu Wen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种连续时间聚合模型，用于揭示暖通空调（HVAC）负荷在应对可再生能源波动时的小时内灵活性，并通过改进的仿射变换和鲁棒机会约束处理异质性和不确定性，结合分层调度框架实现大规模HVAC的高效调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于可再生能源出力快速变化，电力系统需要更多的需求侧灵活性资源来提供备用支持。HVAC负荷具有快速响应能力，但其大规模调度面临计算复杂性和环境温度不确定性等挑战，因此需要一种准确且可靠的聚合模型来挖掘其潜在的小时内灵活性。

Method: 提出了一种新型连续时间（CT）聚合模型，设计了新的仿射变换以处理高维可行域中的异构性，并引入分布鲁棒机会约束来建模室外温度的不确定性。为实现模型的可计算性，采用伯恩斯坦多项式样条、多面体投影和线性化变换等一系列重构技术。进一步提出了定制化的分层调度框架，将CT聚合模型应用于电力系统备用调度中。

Result: 案例研究验证了所提CT聚合模型在聚合精度、小时内的灵活性利用以及不确定性处理方面的有效性和可扩展性，能够高效调度大规模HVAC系统以应对可再生能源的不确定性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的连续时间聚合模型结合不确定性建模与高效计算方法，能够准确可靠地聚合大规模异构HVAC负荷，为其参与电力系统备用服务提供了可行方案，提升了需求侧资源在应对可再生能源波动中的作用。

Abstract: Heating, ventilation, and air conditioning (HVAC) loads, with their rapid response capabilities, can provide considerable intra-hour flexibility on the demand side for reserve provision in order to follow the fast variations of renewables. However, scheduling massive HVACs is challenging due to computation complexity and the uncertainty of outdoor temperature. In this paper, we first introduce a novel continuous-time (CT) aggregation model to reveal the potential intra-hour flexibility of HVACs. For accurate aggregation, a new affine transformation is designed to handle the heterogeneity in high-dimensional feasible region. Further, for reliable aggregation in practical environment, the outdoor temperature uncertainty is constructed by distributionally robust chance constrains and integrated into the aggregation model. Secondly, for the tractable calculation of the proposed CT aggregation model, a cascade of tailored reformulation techniques is proposed, including the Bernstein polynomial spline, polytope projection, and linearization transformation. Thirdly, a customized hierarchical dispatch framework is proposed via incorporating the proposed CT aggregation model into reserve provision in power system dispatch, so as to efficiently schedule massive HVACs to cope with the renewable uncertainty. Case studies verify the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed CT aggregation model in aggregation accuracy, intra-hour flexibility utilization, and uncertainty handling.

</details>


### [129] [Leveraging Time-Causal State Variable Aggregation for Real-Time Schedule of Massive Air Conditioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02410)
*Jingguan Liu,Xiaomeng Ai,Shichang Cui,Xizhen Xue,Shengshi Wang,Jiakun Fang,Jinyu Wen,Yang Shi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于时间因果聚合的近似动态规划算法（TCA-ADP），用于大规模空调负荷的高效实时调度，兼顾计算效率与经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空调负荷具有潜力提供灵活性以应对可再生能源、电价和负荷需求的不确定性，但其实时调度因存在大量时序耦合约束和时间因果不确定性而具有挑战性。

Method: 分析了状态变量聚合的时间因果性要求，构建了增强型聚合模型，并将其转化为线性规划问题以优化参数；利用聚合状态变量近似价值函数，结合离线训练与并行闭式解聚实现近最优实时调度。

Result: 案例研究表明TCA-ADP在聚合精度、不确定性处理以及经济性与可计算性之间平衡方面表现良好，具备良好的可扩展性和实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: TCA-ADP算法能有效实现大规模空调负荷的近最优实时调度，为配电系统管理不确定性提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Air conditioner (AC) loads offer promising flexibility for active distribution networks to manage uncertainties, such as those in renewable energy generation, electricity prices, and load demand. However, real-time scheduling of ACs is challenging due to their massive temporal coupling constraints and time-causal uncertainties. To address this, a novel time-causal aggregation-based approximate dynamic programming (TCA-ADP) algorithm is proposed for efficient scheduling. The time-causality requirements for aggregating state variables are first analyzed to align with the real-time sequential decision-making process. Subsequently, an enhanced aggregation model is developed to ensure both high accuracy and adherence to time causality. The aggregation process is further reformulated as a linear program to optimize aggregation parameters and enable tractable computation. Accordingly, the TCA-ADP leverages aggregated state variables to approximate the value function as a new way, balancing computational efficiency and economy against the large value function space of massive ACs. By training the value function offline using historical data, the TCA-ADP efficiently achieves near-optimal real-time scheduling of massive ACs through parallel and closed-form disaggregation. Case studies demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the TCA-ADP, highlighting its aggregation accuracy, uncertainty handling, and the trade-off between economy and tractability.

</details>


### [130] [Multivariable Geometric Laplace Transform and Fault Detection in Distributed-Converter Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02431)
*Francisco Manuel Arrabal-Campos,Francisco G. Montoya,Santiago Sánchez-Acevedo,Raymundo E. Torres-Olguin,Alfredo Alcayde*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何代数Cl(4,0)中二维几何拉普拉斯变换的直流线路故障诊断方法，利用时空分离特性实现对分布式换流站网络中多种故障的检测、定位与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直流线路中分布着大量电力电子换流器，故障信息在传输过程中与线路动态耦合，传统方法难以有效分离时空特征并精确定位故障，因此需要一种能同时处理时空特性的新型数学工具。

Method: 引入定义在几何代数Cl(4,0)交换子代数上的二维几何拉普拉斯变换（时间-空间域到复频-复波数域的映射），该代数同构于Segre双复数，使用两个正交二重向量B_t和B_x作为独立虚数单位；构建线路的时空传输模型及色散关系，结合导纳成形分布式控制，并精确处理离散换流站点的空间采样、混叠效应及次奈奎斯特带内的精确下垂控制实现；设计可检测、定位并分类多种故障（包括注入损耗、并联、传感器和本地控制器故障）的诊断流程，支持多故障同时发生时的自动阶数选择，并区分电站停运与电缆缺陷。

Result: 实现了故障残差在变换域中的时空解耦表示F_f(s_t) e^{-s_x x_f}，其中位置信息显式体现在几何相位项；建立了完整的分布式换流器线路建模与控制框架；诊断链能够准确识别和区分多种单一及复合故障类型；所有结果均通过配套开源实现复现。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何拉普拉斯变换为分布式电力系统中的时空故障诊断提供了新的数学基础和操作工具，其几何嵌入特性使得时空分析自然分离，显著提升了复杂电网中故障定位与分类的能力，具有良好的理论拓展性与工程应用前景。

Abstract: Monitoring a DC line with many distributed power converters is a genuinely spatio-temporal problem: the information about a localized fault travels along the whole conductor and reaches a few measurement points mixed with the dynamics of the line itself. This paper develops a two-dimensional geometric Laplace transform (t,x) -> (s_t,s_x) over a commutative subalgebra of the geometric algebra Cl(4,0), isomorphic to Segre's bicomplex numbers, in which two bivectors B_t and B_x act as independent imaginary units for the temporal and the spatial phase. Because the two phases live in algebraically distinguishable planes, a fault at position x_f leaves a transformed residual that factorizes as F_f(s_t) e^{-s_x x_f}: its temporal nature stays in the first factor and its location can be read as a geometric argument of the second. On this representation we build a transmission-line model of the converter line and its space-time dispersion relation, a distributed control by admittance shaping, including an exact treatment of discrete converter sites (spatial sampling, aliasing, and a per-converter droop realization that is exact on the sub-Nyquist band), and a fault diagnosis chain that detects, localizes and classifies injection-loss, shunt, sensor and local-controller faults, extends to multiple simultaneous faults with automatic order selection, and distinguishes the outage of a plant from a cable defect. As an integral object the transform is known in bicomplex analysis, and with a single independent variable it reduces to the complex Laplace transform; the contribution lies in its geometric embedding and in its operational use for fault diagnosis in distributed-converter networks. All results are reproduced by an accompanying open implementation.

</details>


### [131] [Effective Range and Optimal Frequency of Through-the-Earth Magnetic Induction Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02485)
*Honglei Ma,Erwu Liu,Wei Ni,Jun Zhu,Zhijun Fang,Rui Wang,Xinyu Qu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了地下磁感应通信（MIC）中的有效通信范围，考虑了地下材料引起的涡流损耗影响，并推导出预测最佳载波频率的闭式表达式以最大化通信距离，仿真结果验证了该分析的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究忽略了地下材料引起的涡流损耗对穿透地球（TTE）磁感应通信范围的影响，本文旨在填补这一空白，提供更准确的有效通信范围分析。

Method: 结合地下介质的电导率和介电常数，推导出TTE MIC的有效通信范围及最优载波频率的闭式表达式，并通过有限元仿真进行验证。

Result: 提出了有效MIC范围的解析模型和最优载波频率的闭式表达式，仿真表明最优载波频率可显著提升通信范围，同时优化天线半径也有助于扩展TTE和车辆MIC应用的通信距离。

Conclusion: 考虑涡流损耗后，所提出的模型能更准确地预测TTE MIC的有效通信范围，且通过优化载波频率和天线尺寸可显著提升系统性能。

Abstract: Magnetic induction communication (MIC) is a promising technology for through-the-earth (TTE) communication. Previous studies on the MIC range have often overlooked the impact of eddy losses caused by underground materials. For TTE MIC, significant eddy losses complicate the analysis of the effective MIC range, which is vital for optimizing performance but has never been addressed in the literature. Accounting for the conductivity and permittivity of the underground medium, this paper derives the effective MIC range in TTE MIC, along with a closed-from expression that predicts the optimal carrier frequency to maximize this range. Finite element simulations validate the analysis, demonstrating that the optimal carrier frequency can significantly enhance the MIC range. It is also revealed that optimizing the antenna radius is effective in extending the MIC range for TTE and vehicle MIC applications.

</details>


### [132] [Physics-based Online Adaptive Koopman Model Predictive Attitude Control for Combined Spacecraft with Dynamic Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02534)
*Yicheng Sun,Yueyong Lyu,Yuhan Liu,Yanning Guo,Wei Pan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理的自适应Koopman模型预测控制策略，用于在惯性不确定性和目标主动机动情况下实现组合航天器的姿态稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在处理非线性动力学和时变惯性不确定性时计算负担重且缺乏物理可解释性，因此需要一种更高效、紧凑且具有物理意义的建模与控制方法。

Method: 构建了一种基于四元数的Koopman模型，利用从四元数运动学导出的解析提升函数，并结合梯度下降法实时更新惯性参数；将该自适应线性模型嵌入MPC框架，转化为二次规划问题。

Result: 所提方法显著降低了在线计算负担，实现了递归可行性与区域输入-状态稳定性，在高保真3D仿真中表现出优于传统方法的控制性能。

Conclusion: 该策略在保证控制精度的同时提升了计算效率，适用于存在模型不确定性和外部扰动的复杂航天任务。

Abstract: This paper proposes a physics-based adaptive Koopman Model Predictive Control (MPC) strategy for combined spacecraft attitude stabilization under inertia uncertainties and active target maneuverability. A novel, quaternion-based Koopman model is constructed from a set of analytical lifting functions derived from the quaternion kinematics, which provides a more compact and physically interpretable linear representation of the nonlinear dynamics compared with the conventional black-box EDMD and higher-dimensional DCM-based model. Leveraging the linear structure of this nominal model, a gradient descent-based update law is employed to efficiently identify time-varying inertial uncertainties from real-time input/output data. By integrating this adaptive linear model into the MPC framework, the optimal control problem reduces to a computationally efficient Quadratic Program (QP), thereby significantly lowering the online computational burden compared to nonlinear adaptive MPC. Recursive feasibility and regional input-to-state stability are formally established through the design of terminal ingredients for the MPC. The effectiveness and superiority of the proposed strategy are validated through comparative simulations of an attitude stabilization task for combined spacecraft in a high-fidelity 3D simulator.

</details>


### [133] [Zonotope-Based Active Exposure of Stealthy Deception Attacks in Sensor-Fusion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02587)
*Meiqi Tian,Shuo Li,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对带有未知但有界噪声的传感器融合信息物理系统的隐蔽攻击检测方法，通过控制通道注入辅助扰动以增强正常输出集与攻击输出集之间的分离度，从而实现对被篡改传感器的有效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传感器融合系统中，多个传感器可能同时被隐蔽攻击所破坏，而传统方法难以有效检测此类攻击，因此需要一种能够在存在噪声的情况下仍能可靠检测攻击的机制。

Method: 构建由安全传感器生成的可接受输出集，并为每个攻击假设建立攻击输出集；引入基于滚动时域优化的暴露输入设计框架，通过控制通道注入有界的辅助控制扰动，以扩大可接受输出集与攻击输出集之间的分离趋势；推导出确保可检测性的充分条件，并提供离线暴露预算指导。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在无人机导航系统遭受隐蔽GNSS和LiDAR攻击时，所提方法能够有效检测出被攻击的传感器，并验证了暴露输入设计和预算选择的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效提升传感器融合系统在未知但有界噪声环境下对隐蔽攻击的检测能力，且通过控制通道注入扰动的方式具有实际可行性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the stealthy attack detection for sensor-fusion cyber-physical systems with unknown-but-bounded noises through the control channel. The detection framework is particularly applicable to sensor-fusion scenarios in which multiple suspicious sensors contributing to the fused estimate may be compromised simultaneously. First, we construct an admissible output set using secure sensors and an attack output set for each attack hypothesis. Then, we introduce a receding-horizon optimization framework to design exposure inputs, namely bounded auxiliary control perturbations injected through the control channel, so as to enlarge the separation between the admissible output set and the attack output sets according to the separation tendency. A sufficient detection condition is further derived, showing that set separation guarantees detectability of the compromised sensors. Moreover, an offline exposure budget guidance is developed to support budget selection before online exposure starts. Simulations on a UAV navigation system under stealthy GNSS and LiDAR attacks validate the proposed method.

</details>


### [134] [Economic Model Predictive Control with Policy-Guided Terminal Ingredients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02628)
*Salim Msaad,Robert D. McAllister*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为策略引导型模型预测控制（policy-guided MPC）的新框架，利用已知的次优控制策略构建终端代价和约束，无需依赖稳态假设，从而扩展了传统模型预测控制在无固定工作点系统中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型预测控制依赖稳态假设来保证闭环稳定性，但在没有或不需要固定工作点的系统中应用受限，因此需要一种不依赖稳态的新型控制框架。

Method: 通过一个已知的次优控制策略进行轨迹 rollout 来确定终端区域的中心，并以偏离该中心的偏差作为惩罚项定义终端代价，从而构造出无需稳态或参考轨迹的终端约束和代价函数。

Result: 建立了相对于引导策略的闭环性能保证，适用于有限和无限时域问题，并通过能量管理的数值仿真验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的策略引导型MPC框架成功摆脱了对稳态的依赖，扩大了模型预测控制在更广泛动态系统中的应用范围，同时保持理论上的稳定性和性能保证。

Abstract: Conventional designs for model predictive control typically rely on terminal costs and constraints derived from a steady state to guarantee closed-loop stability and performance. However, this dependence on a steady-state assumption limits the applicability of this control method to systems in which such a fixed operating point is either not available or not desirable. This work introduces a novel framework, termed policy-guided MPC, to address this limitation. Our approach constructs terminal costs and constraints using a known sub-optimal control policy. Specifically, the terminal region is defined around a center determined by a rollout of the policy, and a penalty on deviation from this center is used to define the terminal cost. This method obviates the need for a steady state or reference trajectory. Closed-loop performance guarantees are established relative to the guiding policy, for both finite and infinite horizon problems. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is demonstrated through numerical simulations on an energy management example.

</details>


### [135] [Guidelines for the implementation of power oscillation damping controllers in power converters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02810)
*Javier Renedo,Macarena Martín Almenta,Sergio Martínez Villanueva,Agustín Díaz-García,Antonio Cordón,Davide Gotti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了在电力电子变换器中实现功率振荡阻尼（POD）控制器的指导原则，通过合成测试系统和西班牙技术标准中的系统化方法验证了其有效性，并分析了不同控制策略对抑制大规模电力系统机电振荡的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了有效抑制电力系统中的机电振荡，近年来研究了在电压源变换器（VSC）中使用POD控制器的方法，但其正确实施依赖于参数设置，因此需要制定实际可行的实施指南和合规性标准。

Method: 采用合成测试系统和西班牙监测合规性技术标准（NTS）中的系统化方法，对基于电网跟随控制（GFL）的通用电力电子变换器进行仿真分析，研究了调节有功功率注入（POD-P）、无功功率注入（POD-Q）或两者同时调节（POD-PQ）的POD控制器。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在合成测试系统中所提出的POD控制器合规性标准是有效的，并且在大规模电力系统中得到了验证，证明了合理设计的POD控制器能够有效抑制机电振荡。

Conclusion: 通过适当的合成系统、方法和合规性标准，电力电子变换器中的POD控制器可以在大规模电力系统中有效抑制机电振荡，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: One of the most effective ways to damp electromechanical oscillations in power systems is by means of supplementary controllers attached to the different devices in the power system is by means of power system stabilizers (PSS) in synchronous machines or by means of power oscillation damping (POD) controllers in facilities with power converters. In the recent years, the use of POD controllers in voltage source converters (VSCs) with grid-following (GFL) control has been investigated. Although the potential of POD controllers to help to damp inter-area oscillations in power systems is enormous, their correct implementation is not trivial, because their effectiveness is strongly linked to their settings. This paper provides guidelines for the implementation of POD controllers in power converters for application in real-world power systems. The paper proposes compliance criteria for POD controllers using a synthetic test system and a systematic methodology used in Spanish technical standard for monitoring compliance (NTS), considering practical considerations. The paper also includes numerical examples to illustrate compliance criteria for POD controllers in a synthetic test system. A generic power converter with grid-following (GFL) control is used for the analysis by simulation and POD controllers using modulation of active-power injection (POD-P), reactive-power injection (POD-Q) or both simultaneously (POD-PQ) will be analysed. Results were validated in a large-scale power system. The paper concludes that by using appropriate synthetic systems, methodologies and compliance criteria, POD controllers in power converters could be effective to damp electromechanical oscillation in large-scale power systems.

</details>


### [136] [Tests on the POD-P controller of INELFE Spain-France VSC-HVDC interconnector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02838)
*Javier Renedo,Agustín Diaz-García,Gilles Torresan,Eduardo Lorenzo Cabrera,Antonio Cordón,Silvia Sanz Verdugo,Juan Peiró,Javier Pérez Castro,Gorka Calvo,Aitor Hernández Sautua,Ambroise Petit,Hamza Oukhayi,Samy Akkari,Hani Saad*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了INELFE VSC-HVDC互联系统中POD-P控制器在不同运行模式下的仿真与现场测试，旨在通过提高控制器增益并结合角度差控制（ADC）来增强对欧洲大陆电力系统区域间振荡的阻尼效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升INELFE VSC-HVDC链路对欧洲大陆电力系统中广泛存在的机电振荡（即功率振荡）的抑制能力，西班牙和法国输电系统运营商联合推动改进POD-P控制器性能，并实现其与角度差控制（ADC）协同工作。

Method: 采用广泛的仿真研究和多种运行模式下的现场测试方法，评估提高POD-P控制器增益并将其与ADC控制结合后的系统动态响应和稳定性表现。

Result: 仿真和现场测试结果验证了改进后的POD-P控制器在不同运行模式下能够有效增强对区域间功率振荡的阻尼作用，且系统行为正常，具备实际应用可行性。

Conclusion: 提高INELFE VSC-HVDC链路POD-P控制器的增益并实现其与ADC控制的协同运行，可显著提升其对欧洲大陆电网互区域振荡的抑制能力，为未来高压直流系统参与电网稳定控制提供了实践依据。

Abstract: INELFE interconnector consists of a 2x1000 MW high voltage direct current system based on voltage source converters (VSC-HVDC) interconnecting France and Spain. INELFE VSC-HVDC link is embedded into the high voltage alternating current (HVAC) system. Electromechanical oscillations, also known as power oscillations, are a major concern worldwide. INELFE VSC-HVDC link has specific controllers to damp power oscillations by modulating active (P)- and reactive (Q)-power injections of the VSC converters (POD-P and POD-Q controllers, respectively). The Spanish and French Transmission System Operators (TSOs) carried out a join task force to - Increase the gain of POD-P controller of INELFE VSC-HVDC link. - Make it possible to use the POD-P controller together with angle difference control (ADC) of INELFE VSC-HVDC interconnector. The objective of these modifications is to improve the effectiveness of POD-P controller and, therefore, to increase its contribution to the damping of inter-area oscillations in the Continental Europe (CE) power system. Such changes require (a) extensive simulation studies and (b) extensive tests in different operation modes, in order to ensure the correct behavior of the system. This paper presents simulation studies and field tests on the POD-P controller of INELFE VSC-HVDC interconnector in different modes of operation.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Quantized mereology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01620)
*Thomas Bittner*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子化部分学（quantized mereology），通过将量子信息理论与经典部分学结合，提出使用量子比特（qubits）来表达存在部分关系模糊性的实体之间的信息，相比经典比特能更有效地描述模糊现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决经典部分学在处理模糊部分-整体关系时的局限性，本文试图引入量子信息理论中的概念，以更好地建模和理解存在不确定性或模糊性的系统结构关系。

Method: 基于经典的RCC5形式系统（Qualitative Reasoning中广泛应用的形式体系），系统地研究如何将经典比特转换为量子比特，并利用集合划分表示qubit状态，从而构建量子化的部分学框架。

Result: 成功展示了从经典QR形式系统向量子化版本的扩展路径；证明了经典形式系统可视为量子化系统的特例，并表明qubits在表达模糊部分关系信息方面优于经典bits。

Conclusion: 量子化部分学为处理定性推理中的模糊性提供了新的理论工具，该方法具有推广到更复杂QR系统的潜力，类似于物理中从经典到量子系统的过渡策略。

Abstract: This paper investigates quantized mereology, which merges quantum information theory with classical mereology, the formal analysis of the part-whole relationship. It emphasizes that information about crisp mereological relations is captured by classical bits, whereas mereological vagueness involves the use of quantum bits, or qubits, to capture mereological information. Transitioning from classical bits to qubits offers a new understanding of part-whole relations among entities that are subject to mereological vagueness. This paper methodically studies the quantized mereology that arises from the classical RCC5 formalism, one of the most widely studied formalisms in Qualitative Reasoning (QR), a subfield of Artificial Intelligence. The argument is made that this method can be extended to quantize more sophisticated QR formalisms, mirroring how strategies are used in physics to transition from classical to quantum systems. The study explores: (i) the conversion of bits to qubits while understanding that these (qu)bits convey mereological information; (ii) the representation of qubit states via set partitions, acknowledging that set theory aligns more closely with classical logic than with the complex vector spaces employed in quantum physics; (iii) demonstrating that classical QR formalisms can be seen as special cases within quantized formalisms; and (iv) showing that qubits more effectively encapsulate information about vague phenomena.

</details>


### [138] [Inverse planning of social interactions in relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01639)
*Alicia M. Chen,Ashley J. Thomas,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Rebecca Saxe*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种形式化模型，将社会关系视为社会环境中的约束条件，类似于物理环境中的边界或障碍，并在同一生成模型中运作但施加不同的行为限制。通过六项预注册实验（N = 1,554），验证了人际关系的正式性与亲密性如何影响人们对人际脆弱行为的解释，揭示了社会关系如何制约并体现在日常行为理解中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在形式化社会关系如何系统地影响人们对他人行为的理解，特别是人际关系中的形式性与亲密性维度在日常情境中的作用机制。

Method: 基于社会科学多领域研究成果，构建一个生成模型，将社会关系（如正式性与亲密性）作为行为解释的约束条件，并在自然主义的日常情境故事中进行检验，通过多个预注册实验收集数据并分析模型对欲望、物理环境和社会关系推断的预测能力。

Result: 模型成功捕捉到人们在六项实验中对欲望、物理环境和社会关系的推断，验证了社会关系作为行为约束的有效性，特别是在解释人际脆弱行为时，形式性与亲密性的差异显著影响理解方式。

Conclusion: 社会关系以结构化的方式约束并揭示日常行为理解，该模型为社会认知提供了统一的计算框架，表明关系结构如同物理环境边界一样，在行动解释中发挥关键作用。

Abstract: We propose a formal account of how structured, shared knowledge about social relationships shapes action interpretation. The model represents relationships as constraints in a social environment, analogous to boundaries or obstacles in a physical environment and operating within the same generative model, but exerting distinct constraints on action. As an initial test of this framework, we draw on research across the social sciences to capture in the models how one dimension of relationships -- formality versus intimacy -- shapes how people interpret interpersonally vulnerable behavior. We test this account in stories of naturalistic everyday situations, extending structured models of action understanding to open-ended contexts. Across six preregistered experiments (N = 1,554), the model captures people's inferences about desires, physical environments, and social relationships. This work formalizes how relationships can constrain -- and be revealed through -- everyday action.

</details>


### [139] [How Fast Do Agents Rot? An Empirical Study of Long-Horizon Degradation in LLM Agents for Production Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01660)
*Shubhra Mittal*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型代理在长程多步工作流中表现不可靠的问题，发现任务成功率随步骤数呈几何级下降，且该现象主要由每步可靠性不足引起，而非上下文长度限制。实验涵盖九种模型、多种任务和情境，结果表明现有模型在较长流程中必然失败，强调需采用面向任务 horizon 的评估与可靠性规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基准测试成功率持续上升，但大语言模型代理在实际生产中的长程多步任务上仍不可靠。本文旨在揭示这一差距的根本原因，特别是任务时域（horizon）的影响。

Method: 通过大规模受控研究，分析九个模型（包括六个开源模型和三个商用系统）、四类任务（含具身代理工具使用循环）、五种任务时域和三种上下文设置下的表现，共分析10,664条轨迹，量化每步可靠性并检验上下文长度的影响。

Result: 任务成功遵循由单个每步可靠性参数决定的几何衰减规律；该参数随模型规模增大而提升，但在所有模型中均低于1，导致长时域下必然失败；在代理性任务中，所有模型在16步内从接近完美降至接近零成功率；限制上下文窗口反而加剧衰减，说明‘迷失在中间’并非主因。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型代理在长程任务中存在根本性可靠性瓶颈，仅依赖整体通过率评估不足以反映真实性能。建议采用基于任务时域的评估方法和可靠性预算机制，以提升生产系统的可预测性与稳定性。

Abstract: Production deployments of large language model (LLM) agents remain unreliable on long, multi-step workflows even as benchmark success rates climb steadily. We argue this gap is largely an artifact of task horizon: benchmarks are dominated by short-to-medium horizons where success remains high, while production workloads demand an order of magnitude more dependent steps. We measure the effect directly, characterizing the shape of agent degradation and disentangling its cause across a large controlled study spanning nine models, six open models from 1.2B to 671B parameters, and three deployed proprietary systems; four task families, including a genuinely agentic tool-use loop; five horizons; and three context regimes. Task success follows a geometric law governed by a single per-step reliability parameter, which rises with model scale but saturates well below 1 even for the strongest models, guaranteeing eventual collapse at sufficiently long horizons. The effect is sharpest on the agentic task, where every model tested, including widely deployed systems, falls from near-perfect success to near zero within sixteen steps of (n=10,664 analyzed trajectories. Degradation is driven by step count rather than context length: bounding the context window steepens decay rather than easing it (logit slope -0.69 vs. -0.44), p=3x10-6), contradicting a lost-in-the-middle explanation and warning against a common production shortcut. Projecting measured reliability onto representative benchmark horizons quantifies a substantial gap between benchmark and production conditions, from 0.42 at GAIA-length horizons to 0.24 at hundred-step production horizons. For teams responsible for agent orchestration and reliability at scale, these results argue for horizon-aware evaluation and reliability budgeting in place of aggregate pass-rate metrics. Code, prompts, seeds, and raw trajectories are released.

</details>


### [140] [Simultaneous Holotheticity and Bi-Hamiltonian Structures in Economic Growth Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01661)
*Sarah Finkle,Roman G. Smirnov*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个严格的几何框架，将有限维双哈密顿结构和南布-泊松力学的形式架构应用于宏观经济增长理论，提出了三种基本经济增长模式的统一分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越静态经验相关性，从几何动力系统的角度理解宏观经济生产函数的本质及其演化规律。

Method: 应用Ryuzo Sato的同时全纯性原理，结合双哈密顿结构与南布-泊松力学，构建可积流的几何叶层模型，并分析资源承载力边界对经济轨迹的影响。

Result: 发现了三种依次推广的基本经济增长机制：经典的Cobb-Douglas模式、资源受限的S形响应模式、容量受限的‘超调-崩溃’模式；并揭示了资源上限导致相容反变泊松笔的灾难性秩坍缩。

Conclusion: 宏观经济生产函数可被视为可积流的稳定几何叶层，资源承载极限会引发结构性危机和动力学框架的崩溃，从而为经济增长的可持续性提供新的数学解释。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a rigorous geometric framework that applies the formal architecture of finite-dimensional bi-Hamiltonian structures and Nambu-Poisson mechanics to macroeconomic growth theory. Moving beyond static empirical correlations, we deploy Ryuzo Sato's principle of simultaneous holotheticity to demonstrate that economic production functions emerge natively as stable, time-independent geometric leaves of integrable flows. We construct a unified, hierarchical taxonomy of three fundamental economic growth regimes that sequentially generalize one another: the classical, unconstrained Cobb-Douglas mode; the resource-limited S-shaped econsystem response; and the capacity-bounded "overshoot-and-collapse" regime. Furthermore, we push this paradigm into non-smooth territory by investigating the structural boundary crises that occur when aggregate economic trajectories encounter definitive resource carrying capacity ceilings. We show that these capacity limits induce a catastrophic rank-collapse of the compatible contravariant Poisson pencil.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Modeling Trade Durations under Temporal Granularity Effects in Forex Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02660)
*Vladimír Holý*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的GA-ACD模型，用于捕捉高频外汇数据中交易持续时间在整数值附近的聚集现象，实证表明该模型优于标准的广义gamma ACD模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频外汇数据中的交易持续时间在整数值附近出现频率增加，现有模型难以准确刻画这一现象。

Method: 基于得分驱动框架，构建了一个两成分混合分布的GA-ACD模型，其中一个成分用于正常持续时间，另一个成分则重新分配整数附近的概率质量以捕捉堆叠效应。

Result: 模拟研究表明忽略堆叠会导致参数估计偏差和推断失真；实证分析显示主要货币对普遍存在整数持续时间聚集，且GA-ACD模型表现优于标准模型。

Conclusion: GA-ACD模型能更准确地刻画高频交易持续时间的整数聚集特征，提升了对持续时间分布与动态特性的建模精度。

Abstract: Trade durations in high-frequency foreign exchange data exhibit increased occurrence near integer values. To address this empirical phenomenon, we propose the granularity-adjusted autoregressive conditional duration (GA-ACD) model. It is based on a novel two-component mixture distribution consisting of a standard generalized gamma component for regular durations and a second component that locally redistributes probability mass around integer values to capture heaping. Conditional dynamics are modeled within a score-driven framework, allowing the scale parameter to vary over time in response to past durations, and enabling maximum likelihood estimation of all model parameters. A simulation study shows that ignoring heaping leads to biased parameter estimates and distorted inference regarding both the distribution and the dynamics of durations. An empirical analysis demonstrates that integer-duration clustering is pervasive across major currency pairs and that the GA-ACD model outperforms the standard generalized gamma ACD model.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Smeared spectral functions from lattice QCD: opportunities and challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02239)
*Shoji Hashimoto*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文综述了从欧几里得关联函数等输入中重建或约束涂抹谱函数的近期发展，讨论了涂抹谱可观测量的机会及主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于逆问题本质上是不适定的，难以获得完全可靠、模型无关且具有可控不确定性的结果，因此需要总结近期发展并探讨解决方案。

Method: 总结了近年来提出的多种方法，并讨论了这些方法在处理涂抹谱函数时的优势和局限性。

Result: 指出了当前在重建或约束谱函数方面的主要挑战，包括逆问题的不适定性以及不确定性控制的困难。

Conclusion: 尽管已有多种方法被提出，但要实现可靠的、模型无关的结果仍面临重大挑战，未来需进一步研究以克服这些问题。

Abstract: A broad class of physical observables can be expressed in terms of smeared spectral functions. Important examples include the hadronic vacuum polarization contribution to the muon $g-2$, hadronic $τ$ decays, inclusive semileptonic $B$ and $D$ decays, and amplitudes for rare processes such as $B\to K^{(*)}\ell^+\ell^-$. In recent years, a variety of methods have been proposed to reconstruct or constrain such spectral functions from Euclidean correlators and related inputs. However, the inverse problem is intrinsically ill-posed, making it difficult to obtain fully reliable, model-independent results with controlled uncertainties. In this contribution, I summarize recent developments, discuss the opportunities offered by smeared spectral observables, and highlight the main challenges that remain.

</details>


### [143] [Probing Criticality Using GMM-Based Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02522)
*Shashank Sharma,Dipankar Chakrabarti,Vipul Arora*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 提出了一类基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的标量势，其采样效率与具有相同对称性的自旋模型相当，同时能再现目标理论的关键性质，适用于研究自发对称性破缺现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋模型虽易于采样，但由于抑制了径向涨落，无法用于研究如Higgs现象等自发对称性破缺问题。因此需要构造既能高效采样又能保留关键物理性质的标量模型。

Method: 基于高斯混合模型（GMM）设计具有特定对称性的标量势，使其属于目标理论的普适类，并通过数值实验验证其普适性。

Result: 成功构建了具有Z2对称性、U(1)规范对称性和无序系统的GMM标量势模型，并通过数值实验证实二维Z2模型属于二维Ising普适类。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持高效采样的同时，能够准确再现临界现象，为研究对称性破缺提供了新的计算工具。

Abstract: Spin models with a given symmetry are easier to sample than scalar theories with the same symmetry on a lattice, as the constrained nature of spin variables enables cheap heat-bath updates. However, this constraint suppresses radial fluctuations, and consequently, spin models cannot be used to study the phenomena of spontaneous symmetry breaking, such as the Higgs phenomenon. To address this, we propose a class of scalar potentials based on Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) that are as easy to sample as spin models with a given symmetry. These potentials can be designed to belong to the same universality class as the theory of interest, thereby reproducing its critical properties while enabling efficient sampling. We construct such models for global $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry, $U(1)$ gauge symmetry, and disordered systems. We also verify by numerical experiment that the case of $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry in two dimensions lies in the two-dimensional Ising universality class.

</details>


### [144] [Double Parton Distributions in the nucleon: Lattice parameter dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02752)
*Daniel Reitinger,Christian Zimmermann,Andreas Schäfer*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本研究通过使用CLS合作生成的Wilson-Clover费米子规范系综，扩展了对格点上双部分子分布（DPD）的研究，重点分析了有限体积效应、格点间距依赖性以及质量参数的影响，以更精确地理解高亮度LHC下BSM物理搜索中的多部分子相互作用背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在高亮度LHC中充分搜寻超出标准模型（BSM）的物理现象，需要更精确地理解所有标准模型背景，尤其是难以实验分离的双部分子相互作用（DPI）和多部分子相互作用（MPI）。因此，通过格点QCD计算双部分子分布（DPD）具有重要意义。

Method: 采用CLS合作生成的Wilson-Clover费米子规范系综，系统研究有限体积效应、格点间距依赖性以及夸克质量参数对DPD的影响，并在多个系综上进行对比分析，以控制系统误差。

Result: 成功评估了有限体积、格点间距和质量参数对DPD计算结果的影响，为进一步实现物理参数下的外推和高精度格点计算奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过多组规范系综的系统性分析可以有效控制格点计算中的系统误差，为未来在物理夸克质量、大体积和小格距下实现精确的DPD计算提供了可行路径，有助于提高对MPI背景的理解精度。

Abstract: To make full use of the HL-LHC, especially in the search for BSM physics, all Standard Model backgrounds have to be understood with increased precision. This is especially difficult for Double Parton Interactions (DPIs) and more generally Multi Parton Interactions (MPIs), which are very difficult to isolate precisely in experiment. DPIs are parameterized by Double Parton Distributions (DPDs) and the lattice determination of the latter is, therefore, especially attractive. In our previous work on DPDs on the lattice, we focused on the results obtained from one gauge ensemble with higher than physical pion mass. In this work, we extend our efforts by investigating artifacts introduced by finite volume and the dependence on the lattice spacing. Moreover, we consider the dependence on the mass parameters. This study is carried out by employing the Wilson-Clover fermion gauge ensembles generated by the CLS collaboration.

</details>


### [145] [On the equivalence of left-hand-cut and three-body formalisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02840)
*André Baião-Raposo,Raul Briceño,Sebastian M. Dawid*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文建立了两种解决格点QCD中“左手割问题”的方法之间的解析和数值等价性，并在一个标量模型中验证了它们能重现相同的无限体积散射振幅，同时指出被忽略的指数压制有限体积效应在小格点体积下可能显著影响振幅提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点QCD中因交换奇点导致标准方法失效的左手割问题，澄清不同提出方法之间的关系。

Method: 通过解析推导和数值模拟，比较一种三体方法与两体左手割形式体系在标量模型中的表现，该模型描述一个粒子通过单粒子交换与S波双粒子束缚态散射。

Result: 两种方法在无限体积散射振幅和左手割结构的编码上完全一致，但在小体积下被忽略的有限体积效应可能数值上不可忽略。

Conclusion: 所研究的两种方法在处理左手割问题上等价，但需谨慎对待小体积下的有限体积效应，以确保振幅提取的准确性。

Abstract: The "left-hand cut problem" in lattice QCD arises when exchange singularities invalidate standard relations used to extract scattering amplitudes from the finite-volume spectrum. In recent years, several approaches were proposed to resolve this problem. We establish analytically and verify numerically the equivalence between the three-body approach proposed in [JHEP 06 (2024) 051] and the two-body left-hand cut formalism developed in [JHEP 08 (2024) 075]. We test the equivalence in a scalar model describing a particle scattering from an $S$-wave two-particle bound state through one-particle exchange. Both approaches reproduce the same infinite-volume particle--bound-state amplitude and correctly encode the associated left-hand cut in the finite-volume spectrum. Crucially, we identify that the exponentially suppressed finite-volume effects neglected in both formalisms can be numerically large, compromising amplitude extraction at small lattice volumes.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Risk-Sensitive Reward Composition for Conditional GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01929)
*Carine Ribeiro dos Santos,Ina Pöhner*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件生成流网络（GFlowNet）的结构化药物设计方法，通过CVaR和最坏情况评分规则构建奖励函数，以应对蛋白质柔性构象带来的挑战，并在合成环境中验证了采样器的有效性与探索策略的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的药物设计方法难以处理蛋白质的多种构象及其生物学上的关键约束，尤其是当候选分子在某一构象中失败时应被直接淘汰而非仅降低评分。现有奖励机制无法满足这一需求，因此需要一种能综合多个打分并体现风险规避的新方法。

Method: 采用条件生成流网络（conditional GFlowNet），结合条件风险价值（CVaR）、最坏情况得分规则和模糊半径来构建鲁棒的奖励函数，定义一个目标家族并进行联合建模；在可完全枚举的合成世界中训练模型，使用精确误差评估性能，并引入exact-KL oracle区分优化器与架构的影响，比较不同探索策略的效果。

Result: 该方法相比平均打分策略能将2到10倍更多的概率质量分配给通过所有构象的候选分子；单个网络可在0.37–2.7倍理想采样器误差范围内覆盖整个目标家族；exact-KL oracle有助于诊断性能瓶颈；当优质候选稀少时，主动注入未见状态的探索策略可发现98.7%-100%的良好区域，而仅重加权已访问状态则只能发现35%-76%。

Conclusion: 基于CVaR和最坏情况原则的奖励构建方式更适合处理具有严格约束的多构象药物设计问题，条件GFlowNet能够有效泛化至目标家族，且合理的探索策略对发现稀有优良解至关重要。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) for structure-based drug design condition on one rigid protein structure. A flexible target holds several distinct structural shapes, its conformations, each occupied for a fraction of the simulation time. Scoring a candidate against all of them raises an open question: how do K scores become one reward? The designer cannot choose arbitrarily. Populations carry simulation error, and biology dictates which conformations are deal-breakers, so a candidate that fails one is disqualified, not merely ranked lower. No standard rule captures this. We compose the reward from a conditional value-at-risk (CVaR), a worst-case score rule, and an ambiguity radius expressing distrust in the stated weights. Together, these define a family of targets, amortised by a single conditional GFlowNet. We answer whether such a sampler can be trained on fully enumerable synthetic worlds, where every error is exact rather than estimated. Pricing the tail rather than averaging moves 2-10 times more mass to candidates that pass every conformation. One network covers the family to within 0.37-2.7x the error of a perfect sampler. An exact-KL oracle, a copy trained on the true target, shows if a shortfall is the optimiser's or the architecture's. When good candidates are rare, exploration decides: injecting unseen states finds 0.987-1.000 of good regions, while reweighting visited finds 0.35-0.76.

</details>


### [147] [A physics-enhanced bidirectional multi-order graph fusion network for interpretable bearing remaining useful life prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02190)
*Haoxuan Zhang,Dinghao Yang,Kangning Zhang,Shaoyong Guo,Haisheng Li,Rui Yang,Ruijun Liu*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理增强的双向多阶图融合网络（PE-BMGN），用于可解释的轴承剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测，结合前向与后向退化序列信息，引入多阶图传播器和门控交叉融合机制，并利用动态记忆库和Kolmogorov-Arnold网络提升模型性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在建模非线性退化过程和模型可解释性方面存在不足，且“黑箱”特性影响工程应用中的可靠性，因此需要一种兼具高精度与可解释性的RUL预测方法。

Method: 提出PE-BMGN模型：1）采用双向结构挖掘前向与后向退化序列的互补信息；2）设计多阶图传播器捕捉局部-全局退化依赖；3）引入门控交叉融合机制动态融合双向特征；4）使用动态记忆库存储历史退化原型以指导预测；5）采用Kolmogorov-Arnold网络实现非线性映射的可视化；6）设计物理增强的动态损失函数提升表示学习的可靠性。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上实验表明，该方法相比现有方法具有最低的预测误差和更保守的估计结果，同时提供了对模型学习过程的可视化解释能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法在提升轴承RUL预测精度的同时增强了模型的可解释性和可靠性，通过融合物理知识与历史退化模式，为工业智能维护提供了更具实用价值的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of bearing remaining useful life (RUL) is a key challenge for intelligent maintenance. Although deep learning-based prediction methods have showed effectiveness, existing methods still have limitations in learning nonlinear bearing degradation processes and model interpretability. Especially in engineering applications, the "black box" nature of deep learning models can easily raise concerns about their reliability. Therefore, we propose a physics-enhanced bidirectional multi-order graph fusion network for interpretable bearing RUL prediction. Our network mines complementary information from both forward and backward degradation sequences. Specifically, our network introduces a multi-order graph propagator to capture the local-global degradation dependencies. A gated cross-fusion mechanism is further designed to dynamically balance the feature contributions from both forward and backward directions. Then, our network stores representative historical degradation prototypes in dynamic memory, so that the final RUL prediction no longer depends solely on the current latent features, but is guided by reusable historical degradation knowledge. To reveal how our model learns the nonlinear degradation process, the feature mapping parts utilize the Kolmogorov-Arnold network, which allows the nonlinear mapping to be visualized using learnable functions. Finally, a physics-enhanced dynamic loss function is developed to help our network learn effective and reliable degradation representations. Extensive experiments on two public datasets show that our method achieves the lowest error while providing more conservative estimates than existing methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/IMGresearcher/PE-BMGN.

</details>


### [148] [A meshfree solver for coupled bulk-surface problems with self-organizing surface geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02489)
*Lennart J. Schulze,Alejandra Foggia,Ivo F. Sbalzarini*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全无网格的数值求解器，用于解决具有可变形界面的耦合体-表面问题，能够隐式追踪表面并求解与周围流体相耦合的动态表面几何和非线性反应-扩散过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多系统中，可变形表面与周围流体的相互作用与表面内的时空动力学紧密耦合，传统方法难以高效处理此类问题，因此需要一种更灵活、自适应的数值方法。

Method: 采用完全无网格方法，隐式追踪表面，基于应力平衡与周围流体相耦合来求解动态表面几何，并同时求解体相中的不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程与表面内的非线性反应-扩散方程。

Result: 在质量守恒的生长球面上验证了方法的收敛性，成功求解了多个耦合体-表面问题，并展示了生物形态发生模型中表面形状与曲面场的双向耦合模拟。

Conclusion: 该无网格求解器能有效处理复杂耦合的可变形界面问题，具有良好的收敛性和应用前景，特别适用于生物形态发生等多物理场耦合系统。

Abstract: In many systems, the interaction between a deformable surface or interface and the surrounding bulk fluid is coupled with intrinsic spatiotemporal dynamics within the moving surface. Examples include tumor growth, biological tissue morphogenesis, cardiac mechanics, multi-phase surfactant chemistry, additive manufacturing, clothing wear-and-tear, and reactive combustion flows. Solving such problems requires both geometric computing algorithms to track and resolve the surface and numerical methods to solve the coupled governing equations in the surface and the surrounding bulk phase. Here, we present a fully meshfree numerical solver for such coupled bulk-surface problems with deformable interfaces. The presented solver tracks the surface implicitly, solving for the dynamic surface geometry based on stress balance coupled to surrounding fluid phases. We show convergence for a mass-conserving case on a growing sphere and solve bulk-surface problems with incompressible Navier-Stokes fluids coupled to in-surface nonlinear reaction-diffusion dynamics. Finally, we show a model of biological morphogenesis, solving simultaneously for the dynamic surface shape and the fields on the curved surface with two-way coupling.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Spectral Analysis of Gram Matrices with Missing at Random Observations: Convergence, Central Limit Theorems, and Applications in Statistical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02024)
*Huiqin Li,Guangming Pan,Yanqing Yin,Wang Zhou*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在随机缺失观测背景下，Gram矩阵的谱性质，建立了经验谱分布的收敛性及其线性谱统计量的中心极限定理，并揭示了缺失机制对谱分布二阶性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于在随机缺失数据下使用Gram矩阵进行统计推断的需求，研究其谱性质具有重要意义。

Method: 在高维框架下分析Hadamard型随机矩阵的谱分布，建立其经验谱分布的极限行为及线性谱统计量的中心极限定理。

Result: 证明了经验谱分布在高维情形下收敛到一个确定的极限分布；建立了线性谱统计量的中心极限定理；发现即使在高斯情形下，缺失机制仍使特征值统计量的波动受总体协方差矩阵特征向量影响。

Conclusion: 随机缺失机制显著影响Gram矩阵的谱统计性质，特别是其二阶波动行为，这一现象不同于经典情况，且可用于协方差矩阵的假设检验。

Abstract: Motivated by the statistical inference using the Gram matrix in the context of missing at random observations, this paper investigates the spectral properties of the random matrices $\mb S_n=\frac{1}{n}\mb Z\mb Z^*$, where $\mb Z=\mb D\circ(\boldsymbol{Σ^{1/2}}\mb X)$ represents a Hadamard random matrix with entries determined by independent Bernoulli variables $\mb D$. Operating within the high-dimensional framework, we establish the convergence of the empirical spectral distribution of $\mb S_n$ to a well-defined limiting distribution. In addition, we explore the impact of the missing mechanism on the second-order properties of the spectral distribution of the Gram matrix $\mathbf{S}_n$. We establish the central limit theorem for the linear spectral statistics of $\mathbf{S}_n$, shedding light on their fluctuations. Surprisingly, our analysis reveals that even in the ideal Gaussian distribution scenario, the fluctuations of statistics generated by eigenvalues are influenced by the eigenvectors of the population covariance matrix in the missing-at-random case. This discovery uncovers a remarkable phenomenon that starkly contrasts with the classical case. Subsequently, we demonstrate the practical application of our central limit theorem in hypothesis testing for the population covariance matrix.

</details>


### [150] [Tests of graph homogeneity, subgraph counts, and quasirandomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02214)
*Rudolf Grübel*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于子图计数的同质随机图（Erdős-Rényi图）的拟合优度检验，利用图函数分解揭示了统计量在零假设下的极限分布，并结合拟随机性结果提出了对所有备择假设一致有效的检验方法，通过模拟验证了有限样本下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了检验同质随机图模型的拟合优度，需要发展基于子图计数的统计检验方法，并理解其在极限情况下的行为。

Method: 使用图函数分解技术分析子图计数检验统计量的极限零分布，结合拟随机性理论构造具有一致性的检验，并通过模拟研究有限样本性能。

Result: 得到了检验统计量的极限分布，提出了一种对所有备择假设都一致有效的检验方法，并在两类常用参数子族上评估了其他检验的表现。

Conclusion: 基于子图计数的检验在理论上具有良好的渐近性质，所提出的检验方法具有一致性，模拟结果验证了其在有限样本下的有效性。

Abstract: Homogeneous random graphs, also known as Erd{\H os}-Rényi graphs, are a subset of the family of dense random graphs, specified by a graphon. We analyze several goodness-of-fit tests for these models that are based on subgraph counts. To obtain the limiting null distribution of the test statistics we use a decomposition of graph functionals, which reveals a cancellation effect. Motivated by a quasirandomness result we obtain a test that is consistent against all alternatives, and we use two popular parametric subfamilies to evaluate the other tests. The theoretical results refer to the limit $n\to \infty$ of the size $n$ of the graph, the behavior for finite $n$ is illustrated by simulations.

</details>


### [151] [Probability-Maximizing Change Detection: Finite-Window Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02570)
*Ali Tajer,Javad Heydari*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了概率最大化序列变点检测，通过在变化后指定时间窗口内停止的概率来衡量性能，并提出了基于截断Shiryaev-Roberts统计量的最优停时规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法以期望检测延迟为性能指标，而本文采用在变化后固定区间内成功检测的概率作为目标，更适用于要求及时响应的应用场景。此外，扩展至多段非重叠瞬态变化情形更具现实意义。

Method: 引入生存加权平均成功准则，将其表示为归一化的期望截断Shiryaev-Roberts（TSR）统计量，并通过最优停时理论推导出具有有限记忆的最优停止规则，该规则使用状态依赖的持续边界进行判断。

Result: 证明了所提准则可精确表示为归一化TSR统计量的期望，得到了最优停止规则；该规则聚合最近窗口内所有可能变点的似然比证据，并与状态相关的阈值比较。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架在有限样本下实现了对瞬态变点的最优检测，适用于需在变化后指定时间内快速响应的多阶段监测问题。

Abstract: This paper investigates probability-maximizing sequential change detection, a formulation in which performance is measured by the probability of stopping within an admissible interval of duration $ξ$ after a change rather than by the expected detection delay. Earlier work introduced this viewpoint in a Bayesian setting and subsequently formalized it under Lorden- and Pollak-type minimax criteria for the case in which successful detection must occur on the \textbf{first} post-change observation, i.e., $ξ=1$. This paper generalizes this framework in two directions. First, the decision maker is allowed to stop within a prescribed window of $ξ\in\mathbb{N}$ post-change observations. Second, the monitored process is allowed to experience multiple, non-overlapping transient change episodes with unknown onset times and durations, so that success consists of stopping within the admissible window associated with any one of these episodes. False alarms are controlled through an average run-length constraint.
  For exact finite-sample analysis, the paper introduces a survival-weighted average success criterion, which represents the probability of successfully detecting a randomly encountered change opportunity conditional on the detector being active at its onset. It is established that this criterion admits an exact representation as the expected \textbf{truncated} Shiryaev--Roberts (TSR) statistic at the stopping time normalized by the average run length, and it characterizes its exactly optimal stopping rule. The optimal procedure has finite memory and aggregates the likelihood-ratio evidence corresponding to all possible change onsets within the most recent window and compares the resulting TSR statistic with a \textbf{state-dependent} continuation boundary obtained from an optimal-stopping formulation.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>
