### [152] [Evolution from Kitaev to XXZ spin chains via distortion: Application to BaCo$_2$V$_2$O$_8$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01805)
*Philip Richard,Mandev Bhullar,Hae-Young Kee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合微观理论与八面体畸变效应，统一解释了BaCo₂V₂O₈中自旋轨道耦合导致的磁各向异性，揭示了g张量与交换相互作用在临界场各向异性中的协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解BaCo₂V₂O₈在横向磁场下异常磁行为（如阶梯磁化和临界场强各向异性）的微观起源，并统一现象学g张量模型与基于自旋轨道耦合的微观交换模型。

Method: 扩展微观理论以包含CoO₆八面体的晶格畸变，分析其对g张量和键依赖交换相互作用（Heisenberg、Kitaev、Γ项）的影响，并利用点电荷模型计算g张量，研究畸变对 staggered 交换项的补偿效应。

Result: 发现八面体畸变不仅产生依赖于位点的各向异性g张量，还通过畸变诱导的贡献部分抵消Kitaev来源的 staggered 项，从而重正化交换相互作用；点电荷计算表明，临界场的强各向异性是修正后的交换作用与各向异性g张量共同作用的结果。

Conclusion: 建立了统一的微观框架，阐明了自旋轨道耦合Ising链材料中磁各向异性的来源，将现象学g张量与微观交换机制联系起来，为理解量子磁性材料提供了更完整的图像。

Abstract: BaCo$_2$V$_2$O$_8$ is a prototypical spin-orbit-coupled Ising-chain antiferromagnet that provides a unique platform for studying field-induced quantum magnetism. Under a transverse magnetic field, it exhibits unusual magnetic properties, including an anomalous staggered magnetization and a pronounced anisotropy of the critical field with respect to the in-plane field direction. While these phenomena have been attributed phenomenologically to a site-dependent anisotropic $g$-tensor, a recent microscopic theory has shown that spin-orbit coupling naturally generates bond-dependent Heisenberg, Kitaev, and $Γ$ exchange interactions. Here, we unify these two pictures by extending the microscopic theory to incorporate distortions of the CoO$_6$ octahedra. We show that the distortions not only generate the site-dependent anisotropic $g$-tensor but also renormalize the staggered exchange interactions through a distortion-induced contribution that partially compensates the Kitaev-derived staggered term. Using point-charge calculations to estimate the $g$-tensor of BaCo$_2$V$_2$O$_8$, we demonstrate that the strong anisotropy of the critical field originates from the combined effects of the modified exchange interactions and the anisotropic $g$-tensor. Our work provides a unified microscopic framework for understanding the magnetic anisotropy of spin-orbit-coupled Ising-chain materials.

</details>


### [153] [Observation of g-wave altermagnetic multipole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01969)
*Ryo Misawa,Rikuto Oiwa,Shunsuke Kitou,Tatsuya Miki,Motohiko Ezawa,Weiyi Yun,Rinsuke Yamada,Chihaya Koyama,J. Alberto Rodríguez Velamazán,Kamil K. Kolincio,Navid Qureshi,Elina Zhakina,Yuiga Nakamura,Jan Masell,Ilya Belopolski,Taka-hisa Arima,Yusuke Nomura,Satoru Hayami,Max Hirschberger*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过高能同步辐射X射线衍射和价电子密度分析，直接可视化了典型反铁磁体CrSb中的g波多重极结构，揭示了其电十六极矩的反铁磁有序，并结合极化中子衍射证实了磁多重极的共存，建立了altermagnetism的微观模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越传统磁体的新型自旋电子学材料，理解altermagnets中时间反演对称性破缺的物理起源及其隐藏的高阶多重极序。

Method: 结合高能同步辐射X射线衍射、价电子密度（VED）分析和极化中子衍射技术，辅以微观模型构建，实现对g波altermagnetic多重极的直接观测与量化。

Result: 在CrSb中观察到交替Cr子晶格间的价电子密度方向各向异性，证实了g波altermagnets中电十六极矩的反铁磁有序；发现其与磁偶极子共存可诱导出铁磁性的磁多重极；建立了g波多重极与g波自旋分裂之间的微观关联模型。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了altermagnetism在实空间中的指纹特征，建立了探测量子材料中隐藏多重极序的通用方法，为理解高阶磁结构和设计新型自旋电子器件奠定了基础。

Abstract: Over the past few years, altermagnets have emerged as a new class of collinear magnets with broken time-reversal symmetry, offering novel opportunities for spintronics beyond conventional magnets. Rather than from net magnetization, as in ferromagnets, the unconventional time-reversal symmetry breaking of altermagnets originates from antiferroic magnetic dipoles locked to higher-order multipoles. Here we report the direct visualization of a $g$-wave altermagnetic multipole in the canonical altermagnet CrSb. Combining high-energy synchrotron X-ray diffraction with valence electron density (VED) analysis, we uncover a pronounced directional anisotropy of the VED distribution alternating between Cr sublattices. This evidences the antiferroic order of electric hexadecapoles predicted in $g$-wave altermagnets. Its coexistence with antiferroic magnetic dipoles induces ferroic magnetic multipoles, as probed by polarized neutron diffraction. We further identify a microscopic model of altermagnetism that directly relates the $g$-wave multipole and the $g$-wave spin splitting. Through direct observation and quantification of multipoles, this study provides a real-space fingerprint of altermagnetism and establishes a general probe of hidden multipole order in quantum materials.

</details>


### [154] [Thermodynamic Electric Toroidal Dipole and Intrinsic Longitudinal Spin Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02443)
*Taisei Yamanaka,Takumi Sato,Satoru Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个热力学电环形偶极子（ETD）的理论框架，作为晶体中ferroaxial相的体序参量，并建立了其与内禀纵向自旋电导率之间的直接关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统多极子算符涉及未明确定义的位置算符，周期性晶体中电环形偶极子的体定义一直难以实现。

Method: 通过将空间变化的电场与相对论性自旋诱导的电极化耦合，构建了具有规范不变性的热力学ETD表达式。

Result: 推导出的ETD表达式是规范不变的，可作为ferroaxial相的体序参量；并发现ETD对化学势的导数与绝缘系统中的内禀纵向自旋电导率直接相关；在Kane-Mele模型的最小ferroaxial扩展中验证了该理论。

Conclusion: 建立了晶体固体中ETD的热力学理论，识别出纵向自旋电导率为ferroaxial有序的直接输运表现。

Abstract: Electric toroidal dipoles (ETDs) characterize ferroaxial order, yet their bulk definition in periodic crystals has remained elusive because conventional multipole operators involve the ill-defined position operator. Here we formulate a thermodynamic ETD by coupling a spatially varying electric field to the relativistic spin-induced electric polarization. The resulting expression is gauge invariant and provides a bulk order parameter for ferroaxial phases. We further establish a direct relation between the chemical-potential derivative of the ETD and the intrinsic longitudinal spin conductivity in insulating systems. To demonstrate the formulation, we construct a minimal ferroaxial extension of the Kane--Mele model. The ETD becomes finite exclusively in the ferroaxial phase and is strongly enhanced near a small band gap, accompanied by a sizable longitudinal spin current. Our results establish a thermodynamic theory of ETDs in crystalline solids and identify the longitudinal spin conductivity as a direct transport manifestation of ferroaxial order.

</details>


### [155] [Prominent Dimensional Effects on High-order Harmonic Generation in Strongly Correlated Electron Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02466)
*Kento Uchida,Shintaro Takayoshi,Yuta Murakami,Dongjoon Song,Alannah M. Hallas,Masayuki Watanabe,Takashi Konishi,Aiko Nakano,Koichiro Tanaka*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了维度对关联量子材料中极端非线性光学响应的影响，通过高次谐波生成实验和Hubbard模型的非平衡模拟，发现维度和自旋-电荷耦合是调控相关绝缘体中极端非线性光学响应的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索维度在关联量子材料中的基本激发对极端非线性光学响应的影响，填补该领域研究空白。

Method: 结合准一维SrCuO$_2$和准二维Pr$_2$CuO$_4$的高次谐波生成实验与Hubbard模型的非平衡模拟进行分析。

Result: 发现SrCuO$_2$表现出稳健的平台状高次谐波谱和弱温度依赖性，而Pr$_2$CuO$_4$则显示谐波产率单调下降且高温下尤其是高阶谐波被强烈抑制；模拟结果揭示了由自旋-电荷耦合主导的维度依赖性双空穴去相位机制。

Conclusion: 维度和自旋-电荷耦合是调控关联绝缘体中极端非线性光学响应的关键控制参数。

Abstract: Dimensionality strongly affects elementary excitations in correlated quantum materials, yet its impact on extreme nonlinear optical responses remains largely unexplored. Here, we combine high-harmonic generation (HHG) experiments on quasi-one-dimensional SrCuO$_2$ and quasi-two-dimensional Pr$_2$CuO$_4$ with nonequilibrium simulations of Hubbard models. We find a pronounced dimensional contrast: SrCuO$_2$ exhibits a robust plateau-like high-harmonic spectrum with weak temperature dependence, whereas Pr$_2$CuO$_4$ shows a monotonic decrease in harmonic yield and strong thermal suppression, especially at higher harmonics. The simulations qualitatively reproduce these trends and identify dimensionality-dependent doublon--holon dephasing, governed by spin--charge coupling, as their microscopic origin. These results establish dimensionality and spin--charge coupling as key control parameters for extreme nonlinear optical responses in correlated insulators.

</details>


### [156] [Disorder-driven competing magnetic interactions and glassy magnetic behavior in quaternary Heusler alloy FeRuMnGe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02506)
*Manikantha Panda,Prabuddha Kant Mishra,Sonali S. Pradhan,Aarti Gautam,Bhagyashree Pol,P D Babu,Ashok Kumar Ganguli,V. Kanchana,Tapas Paramanik*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验与理论结合，揭示了四元赫斯勒合金FeRuMnGe中反位无序对磁性基态的调控作用，发现无序引发竞争交换相互作用，导致簇状玻璃磁态与长程有序共存，并实现了从反铁磁主导到铁磁主导的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过扩展FeRuMnZ（Z = Ga, Si）系列至Ge，探究化学取代如何通过改变原子有序性和电子结构来调控磁性基态。

Method: 采用X射线衍射分析结构无序，结合理论计算研究交换相互作用变化，并通过磁性测量（如交流磁化率和弛豫测量）表征磁行为。

Result: 发现B2型反位无序导致铁磁与反铁磁相互作用竞争，使材料由半金属变为金属；观察到约161 K以下增强的磁响应和最大约1.64 μ_B/fu的磁化强度，且存在无热力学特征的簇玻璃态。

Conclusion: 无序程度直接调控四元赫斯勒合金中的交换作用竞争，进而决定其磁性状态，为理解此类材料中无序与磁性关系提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this combined experimental and theoretical study, we investigate the role of disorder in governing the magnetic ground state of the quaternary Heusler alloy FeRuMnGe. In the FeRuMnZ (Z = Ga, Si) series, chemical substitution modifies atomic ordering and electronic structure, resulting in distinct magnetic ground states. Motivated by this, we extend the series to FeRuMnGe. X-ray diffraction reveals B2-type antisite disorder, where Fe--Ru and Mn--Ge intermix. Theoretical calculations show that such disorder modifies exchange interactions, leading to competing ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic couplings, and drives the system from half-metallic to metallic. Magnetic measurements reveal competing interactions, giving rise to a cluster-glass state coexisting with long-range magnetic order. The absence of a thermodynamic signature at $T_f$, together with ac susceptibility and relaxation measurements, supports the presence of short-range magnetic interactions and a glassy magnetic state. The compound exhibits an enhanced magnetic response below $\sim 161$ K and a maximum magnetization of $\sim 1.64~μ_B$/f.u. at 5 T. Overall, this work establishes a direct correlation between antisite disorder, competing exchange interactions, and glassy magnetism in quaternary Heusler alloys. Combined experimental results and theoretical calculations reveal that disorder drives the system from an AFM-dominated state in FeRuMnSi to an FM-dominated state in FeRuMnGe, providing deeper insight into the role of the extent of disorder in governing the magnetic properties of QHAs.

</details>


<div id='physics.ao-ph'></div>

# physics.ao-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Baseline Climatology of Tornadoes and Waterspouts in the Philippine Archipelago](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01692)
*Generich H. Capuli*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本研究基于菲律宾的龙卷风和水龙捲报告，结合ERA5数据，首次建立了热带岛国环境下此类天气事件的气候基线与环境特征，发现传统中纬度对流参数在热带海洋环境中存在误判风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 龙卷风预警主要依赖于中纬度环境条件，而热带地区的相关研究严重不足，导致现有判据在热带地区适用性存疑。

Method: 收集1900年代至2025年菲律宾1,186例龙卷风与水龙捲事件，结合ERA5再分析资料的邻近探空数据，进行气候统计与环境场特征分析。

Result: 发现事件多集中于三大山脉毗邻走廊，时间集中在5-10月；环境具有高对流有效位能但低层风切变弱，0-6公里风差普遍低于10节，低层水平涡度以流向分量为主（68%-79%），且 hodograph 具有低层曲率。传统复合指数低估实际风险。

Conclusion: 中纬度校准的对流参数不适用于热带海洋环境，需建立本地化阈值以提升预警能力。

Abstract: Tornadoes are documented on every inhabited continent, yet the environmental condition used to anticipate them was developed and validated overwhelmingly on mid-latitude records, leaving tropical regimes largely unexamined. Here, we collected 1,186 tornado and waterspout reports combined in the Philippines spanning the early 1900s to 2025 and paired each with an ERA5 proximity sounding to construct the first baseline climatology and environmental characterization of these severe weather events in the archipelago. Activity concentrates in three corridors, mostly flanked by mountain ranges, one in each major island group, and is sharply phased within a May-October window that closely tracks the annual cycle of thunderstorm frequency. The environments are thermodynamically permissive but kinematically shallow as mean convective available potential energy exceeds typical Great Plains values, while bulk wind difference between the 0-6 km layer stays below 10 kt, diverging from the mid-latitude covariance in which instability and wind shear increase together. Nearly all of the discriminating variance instead resides in the lowest kilometer, where 68-79% of the near-surface horizontal vorticity is streamwise, reaching 96% in the most organized profiles, and the hodographs retain low-level curvature. Consequently, composite indices underestimate even as tornadoes recur, and locally rescaled thresholds are required. Therefore, mid-latitude calibrated convective parameters can misdiagnose tropical-maritime regimes.

</details>


### [158] [Kilometer-Scale AI Downscaling of Atlantic Hurricanes with Generative Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02034)
*Yingkai Sha,Talea L. Mayo,Ethan D. Gutmann,Lulin Xue,Andrew Newman*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于人工智能的动力降尺度系统，用于热带气旋（TC）的高分辨率模拟，结合AI区域模型与扩散模型生成精细化、集合化的极端天气场，验证显示其在多源强迫下均表现稳定且优于确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值模式对小尺度极端天气（如热带气旋）的高分辨率模拟计算成本高，亟需高效、准确的替代方法。

Method: 构建一个两阶段AI系统：第一阶段使用AI局地模型自回归地将低分辨率边界条件（每3小时）降尺度为高分辨率（每小时）气象场；第二阶段利用扩散模型将输出转换为灾害相关变量的集合预报。系统在重网格化的CONUS404数据和ERA5强迫下训练，并在2020–2024年20个TC案例中评估。

Result: 系统在大西洋飓风季表现出稳定的降尺度性能，能量谱接近CONUS404参考数据；在TC相关的极端天气预测上优于确定性AI基线；能捕捉眼墙、雨带和登陆结构细节；且对GDAS/FNL等不同来源强迫数据也具备良好适应性。

Conclusion: 该AI动力降尺度系统可有效生成高分辨率、集合化的热带气旋气象场，为小尺度极端天气模拟提供了高效可行的新范式。

Abstract: This study presents an AI-based dynamical downscaling system for Tropical Cyclones (TCs). The system incorporates an AI-based limited-area model that downscales 3-hourly low-resolution boundary forcings into hourly high-resolution fields autoregressively, and a diffusion model that converts the outputs into ensembles of hazard-relevant variables. The system is trained on the regridded CONUS404 data with ERA5 forcings, and is evaluated on 20 TCs in 2020--2024. Verification shows stable downscaling performance across Atlantic hurricane seasons, with energy spectra closely matching the CONUS404 reference. The system is also verified to produce skillful TC-relevant weather extremes, largely improved over a deterministic AI baseline. The system performs well with forcing data from other models (GDAS/FNL) and can produce detailed eyewall, rainband, and landfall structures in TC case studies. The study provides a good example of how AI-based dynamical downscaling systems can be designed to resolve small-scale extreme weather events.

</details>


### [159] [Did recent sea surface temperature warming reinforce the extreme East Asian summer monsoon precipitation in 2020?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02545)
*Taeho Mun,Haerin Park,Dong-Hyun Cha,Chang-Keun Song,Seung-Ki Min,Seok-Woo Son*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 2020年东亚夏季风降水异常主要并非由近期海表温度（SST）变暖驱动；区域气候模拟显示，暖SST抑制西北太平洋副高西伸并减弱西南气流，导致东亚地区降水减少，其动力效应压制了热力效应带来的增雨趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究近年来海表温度（SST）变暖是否对2020年异常强烈的东亚夏季风（EASM）降水事件有显著影响。

Method: 通过区域气候模式实验，对比使用观测SST与去除22年SST趋势的冷SST情景下的降水和环流变化，分析SST的动力与热力学效应。

Result: 暖SST导致低纬度降水增加但东亚夏季风区降水减少，呈偶极子型降水格局，与2020年实际降水异常相反；同时抑制西北太平洋副高西伸，削弱南中国海向北的西南气流，并增强局地Walker和Hadley环流。

Conclusion: 2020年东亚夏季风降水异常不太可能由近期SST变暖引起；在暖SST下，动力效应导致的降水减少超过热力效应带来的增加，抑制了EASM降雨。

Abstract: We analyzed the possible effects of recent sea surface temperature (SST) warming on the extraordinary East Asian summer monsoon (EASM) precipitation in 2020 summer. The dynamic and thermodynamic impacts of SST are examined by conducting regional climate model experiments with observed SST and cold SST where the 22-year SST trend is removed. In the presence of warm SST, precipitation increases in low latitudes but decreases in the EASM region. This dipolar precipitation change pattern opposes the precipitation anomalies in 2020 summer, indicating that the extraordinary 2020 EASM precipitation is not likely driven by recent SST warming. The warm SST suppresses the western North Pacific subtropical high expansion and weakens the southwesterly from the South China Sea toward the EASM region. In terms of large-scale atmospheric circulations, SST-induced wind changes strengthen the local Walker circulation in the South China Sea and the Philippines and the local Hadley circulation across the EASM region. These support the reduced EASM rainfall in the control experiment compared to the cold SST experiment and imply that the precipitation reduction by dynamical effects could exceed the precipitation increase by thermodynamic effects in the EASM region under warm SST.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [New strategies for some pursuit-evasion differential games with Gronwall-type constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01922)
*Jamilu Adamu,Mehdi Salimi,Farouq Abba Wabi,Jewaidu Rilwan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在序列空间$l_2$中的一类非合作追逃微分博弈，其中追击者和逃避者的运动由一阶微分方程描述，且其控制函数受到Gronwall型约束。提出了新的追击策略和逃避策略，并建立了保证追击成功或逃避成功的充分条件，推广了经典几何和积分约束下的微分博弈结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展经典约束下的微分博弈理论，研究更广泛的Gronwall型历史依赖约束下的追逃问题。

Method: 在序列空间$l_2$中构建满足Gronwall型约束的追击与逃避策略，利用泛函分析和微分不等式方法推导充分条件。

Result: 获得了追击完成和成功逃避的充分条件，提出了有效的追击和逃避策略。

Conclusion: 所提方法推广了已有结果，适用于更广泛的历史依赖约束系统。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of non-cooperative pursuit-evasion differential games in the sequence space $l_{2}.$ The motions of the pursuer and the evader are described by certain first-order differential equations while their control functions are subject to Gronwall-type constraints which impose history-dependent bounds on their control resources. Two fundamental problems are investigated. For the pursuit problem, new strategy for the pursuer is constructed and sufficient conditions guaranteeing the completion of pursuit are established. For evasion problem, an admissible strategy of the evader ensuring avoidance of capture is obtained, and corresponding sufficient conditions for successful evasion are derived. The proposed approached extends existing results on differential games with classical geometric and integral constraints to a broader class of systems governed by Gronwall-type restrictions.

</details>


### [161] [A Hereditary Property of Cutting Plane Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02038)
*Gérard Cornuéjols,Vrishabh Patil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了割平面方法中的“遗传性质”，提出了两个充分条件以判断该性质是否成立，并证明了多种经典割平面闭包满足该性质，同时指出了若干不满足该性质的经典方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管遗传性质在有限秩和闭包多面体性证明中具有重要作用，但尚未在各类割平面方法中被系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，明确哪些常见方法具备该性质。

Method: 提出两个判断割平面方法是否具备遗传性质的充分条件：其一基于格自由凸集族的交割闭包，其二基于Gomory角多面体的有效不等式；并针对多个经典方法进行逐一验证。

Result: 证明分裂、提升投影、Lovász-Schrijver、Sherali-Adams和Lasserre闭包均满足遗传性质；同时发现Gomory分数割、混合整数Chvátal闭包、+-割闭包以及仅由可行基生成的Dantzig割闭包不满足该性质。

Conclusion: 遗传性质在多种重要割平面方法中成立，但并非普遍成立；本文提供的两个充分条件有助于判断新提出的割平面方法是否具备该性质。

Abstract: Let $K'$ denote the closure of a convex set $K$ under a given cutting-plane procedure. The procedure satisfies the \emph{hereditary property} if $F' = K' \cap F$ for every face $F$ of $K$. The property underlies inductive proofs of finite-rank and the polyhedrality of closures, and it is an admissibility requirement in abstract frameworks for cutting-plane procedures. Yet, it has not been studied systematically across well-known cutting-plane procedures. We establish two sufficient conditions for the property. The first applies to procedures that can be expressed as closures under intersection cuts from a family of lattice-free convex sets, when a single family realizes the closure of $K$ and of each of its faces. It yields the hereditary property for the split, lift-and-project, Lovász--Schrijver, Sherali--Adams, and Lasserre closures, applied over general convex sets and for faces that need not be exposed. The second applies to procedures whose cuts are valid inequalities for Gomory's corner polyhedron, and it yields the hereditary property for the closure of Dantzig cuts derived from all bases. We complement these results with four classical procedures for which the hereditary property fails, namely the closure of Gomory's fractional cuts (derived from either all bases or feasible bases only), the mixed-integer Chvátal closure, the $+$-cut closure, and the closure of Dantzig cuts derived from feasible bases.

</details>


### [162] [Stochastic Optimal Control Problem under Inside Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02164)
*Yuxin Ye,Jingtao Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在内部信息下的随机最优控制问题，通过Donsker delta函数和前向积分方法处理非适应过程，并在高斯假设下建立了最优性条件，最后通过数值例子验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于内部信息导致控制过程非适应，传统随机积分无法适用，因此需要引入新的数学工具来处理此类控制问题。

Method: 采用前向积分定义随机微分方程中的积分项，利用Donsker delta函数将原系统转化为参数化的系统，并使用流变换技术证明解的唯一性，在高斯假设下推导最优性条件。

Result: 建立了前向SDE解的存在性和唯一性，得到了内部信息下LQ控制问题的最优性条件，并将问题转化为具有随机系数的LQ控制问题。

Conclusion: 本文成功地处理了基于内部信息的随机最优控制问题，为非适应控制系统提供了有效的分析框架和求解方法。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with a stochastic optimal control problem under inside information. The control process depends on an $\mathcal{F}_{T_0}$-measurable random variable $Y$, representing the static inside information, and is adapted to the enlarged filtration generated by the underlying Brownian motion and the random variable $Y$. Accordingly, the traditional stochastic integral fails to be well-defined in this non-adapted setting; we adopt forward integrals to formulate the stochastic integral terms in the system. By means of the Donsker delta function, the original controlled system is transformed into a $y$-parameterized system. We further establish the existence of solutions to forward \textit{stochastic differential equations} (SDEs), and prove the uniqueness of solutions via flow transformation techniques. Under a Gaussian assumption on $Y$, we derive both necessary and sufficient optimality conditions for the aforementioned control problem. Subsequently, we formulate the \textit{linear-quadratic} (LQ) optimal control problem under inside information. Through the $y$-parameterized transformation, the original problem is converted into an LQ control problem with random coefficients. A numerical example for the LQ case is provided at the end to validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [163] [Characterizations and Complexity of Minimum Forward and Integer Cycle Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02317)
*Gabor Riccardi,Niels Lindner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向图中前向环基的结构与计算复杂性，给出了弱基本和强连通图中前向基本环基存在的完整刻画，并证明最小权前向弱基本环基问题是APX难的；同时引入了“选择加入图”（opt-in graphs）的概念，证明其为闭合于次图的操作且可用多项式时间算法识别，应用该算法得出完全图$K_n$当且仅当$n \leq 7$时是opt-in的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有向图中最短环基相关问题的复杂性开放问题，特别是关于最小权重整数环基和前向基本环基的存在性、结构与可计算性。

Method: 通过图论结构分析、组合优化方法以及L-归约技术，对前向环基的存在性和计算难度进行理论刻画，并引入opt-in图的概念，结合Robertson-Seymour定理设计识别算法。

Result: 1. 刻画了存在弱基本前向环基的有向图结构；2. 证明强连通图存在前向基本环基当且仅当其有向环数等于环秩，此时基唯一；3. 最小权前向基本环基可在多项式时间内求解，但最小权弱基本版本是APX-hard；4. 引入opt-in图并证明其为次图闭合族，提出识别算法；5. 验证$K_n$在$n \leq 7$时为opt-in图。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了有向图中前向环基的基础理论问题，揭示了其结构性质与计算边界，同时提出的opt-in图概念为环基整性研究提供了新框架。

Abstract: The cycle space of a directed graph is generated by a cycle basis, where, in general, cycles are allowed to have both forward and backward arcs. In a forward cycle, all arcs must follow the given direction. Several open questions remain regarding the complexity of the minimum cycle basis problem, in particular the minimum-weight integral cycle basis problem, and the minimum-weight weakly and strictly fundamental forward cycle basis problems. In this paper, we address these open questions. First, we study the existence, structure, and computational complexity of minimum-weight forward cycle bases. We give a complete structural characterization of digraphs that admit weakly fundamental (and hence integral) forward cycle bases. We further provide a characterization when a strongly connected digraph admits a forward fundamental cycle basis, proving that such a basis exists if and only if the set of directed cycles has cardinality equal to the cycle rank; in this case, the basis is unique. Lastly, we show that while minimum-weight forward fundamental cycle bases can be found in polynomial time whenever they exist, the minimum-weight forward weakly fundamental cycle basis problem is APX-hard via an L-reduction from the minimum-weight weakly fundamental cycle basis problem on digraphs with metric weights. Second, we introduce opt-in graphs, i.e., the family of graphs for which minimum cycle bases are integral for any weight function. We show that this family is minor-closed and hence, by the Robertson-Seymour theorem, is characterized by a finite set of forbidden minors, so that the opt-in recognition problem is solvable in polynomial time. Lastly, we present an algorithm to check whether a graph is opt-in, and if not, to identify which of its minors belong to the set of forbidden minors. Applying this algorithm, we show that the complete graph $K_n$ is opt-in if and only if $n \leq 7$.

</details>


### [164] [A simple derivation of the Kalman filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02332)
*Marco Chiani,Giovanni Petris,Moe Z. Win*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文以最小的数学开销推导了离散时间卡尔曼滤波方程，仅需基本的最小二乘估计知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化卡尔曼滤波方程的推导过程，使其更易于理解和教学。

Method: 基于最小二乘估计的方法进行推导，保持严谨性和通用性的同时减少数学复杂度。

Result: 得到了简洁且自包含的离散时间卡尔曼滤波方程推导过程。

Conclusion: 该方法在降低数学门槛的同时，仍保持了理论的严谨和广泛适用性。

Abstract: In this lecture note, we present a concise and self-contained derivation of the discrete-time Kalman filter equations that requires only a basic understanding of least squares estimation. The treatment is designed to minimize mathematical overhead while preserving both rigor and generality.

</details>


### [165] [On the invariance of risk-sensitive LQR gain under input randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02363)
*Yeongjun Jang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在风险敏感线性二次调节器（LQR）问题中，最优增益在输入随机化下保持不变，尽管输入噪声增加了过程噪声，但由于其同时影响系统动态和代价函数，总效应抵消，最优增益不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究输入随机化对风险敏感LQR最优增益的影响，尤其是在隐私保护和探索策略中的应用需求。

Method: 通过分析输入噪声对系统动态和代价函数的联合影响，推导出最优增益在输入随机化下的不变性。

Result: 证明了最优增益及其相关的Riccati递推无需重新计算，且最优成本的增量可闭式求解。

Conclusion: 该结果有助于在需要输入随机化的应用中使用风险敏感LQR，如重放攻击检测的水印技术、差分隐私和路径积分控制。

Abstract: This paper shows that the optimal gain of the risk-sensitive linear quadratic regulator (LQR) problem is invariant under input randomization, i.e., when the controller deliberately injects noise into the nominal control input. This appears counterintuitive at first glance because certainty equivalence does not hold for risk-sensitive LQR and input randomization inflates the effective process noise. Nonetheless, the gain is preserved because the input noise enters not only the system dynamics but also the cost functional, and its total effect on the gain eventually vanishes. Consequently, the optimal gain and its associated Riccati recursion need not be recomputed, and the increment in the optimal cost can be readily evaluated in closed form. This result facilitates the use of risk-sensitive LQR in applications that employ input randomization for privacy or exploration, such as watermarking for replay attack detection, differential privacy, and path integral control.

</details>


### [166] [On the Duality between Feature and Sample Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02418)
*Thu-Le Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fenchel-Rockafellar表示的统一框架，揭示了特征筛选与样本筛选之间的对称等变关系，表明在凸优化问题中，对偶化后的特征筛选等同于样本筛选后的对偶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管特征筛选和样本筛选被视为对偶概念，但二者的关系缺乏形式化、模型无关的理论描述，本文旨在建立一种普适的理论框架来阐明其内在联系。

Method: 通过将筛选操作和对偶性视为目标函数的变换，引入涵盖Lasso和SVM的一类凸问题——Fenchel-Rockafellar表示，并在此框架下分析筛选操作的代数性质。

Result: 证明了特征筛选与样本筛选构成一对等变操作，即对偶化后进行特征筛选等价于先进行样本筛选再对偶化。

Conclusion: 该研究为特征与样本筛选提供了统一的数学视角，揭示了其在凸机器学习模型中的深层对称结构，增强了模型压缩与加速方法的理论基础。

Abstract: Feature and sample screening reduce the cost of machine learning by eliminating irrelevant features and noninformative samples, respectively. Although recognized as primal-dual counterparts, their relationship remains informal and model-dependent. Viewing screening and duality as transformations of objective functions, we introduce Fenchel-Rockafellar (FR) representations, a class of convex problems encompassing the Lasso and SVM that is closed under both transformations. We then prove that feature and sample screening form an equivariant pair: dualization followed by feature screening is equal to sample screening followed by dualization.

</details>


### [167] [Accelerated Convergence of a Second-Order Dynamical System and its Application to Splitting Algorithms for Comonotone Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02479)
*Yan Tang,Jun Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前向-后向分裂算子的二阶动力系统，用于求解希尔伯特空间中由最大ρ-共单调算子A和ν-余强制算子B构成的结构化包含问题，并通过Lyapunov分析证明了加速收敛性，离散化后得到一类双惯性Halpern型算法，在多个数值实验中表现出良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决希尔伯特空间中形如0∈(A+B)(x)的结构化包含问题，特别是当A为最大ρ-共单调算子、B为ν-余强制算子时，现有的一阶方法收敛速度有限，因此需要设计具有加速性质的二阶动力系统以提升性能。

Method: 引入一个由前向-后向分裂算子驱动的二阶微分包含系统，利用Lyapunov函数分析系统的良好定义性与收敛性，并通过对时间进行离散化构造出一类具有双惯性的Halpern型前向-后向分裂算法。

Result: 建立了该动力系统的适定性，获得了o(1/t)量级的速度收敛率和o(1/t²)量级的前向-后向残差收敛率，并证明了轨迹弱收敛到解集zer(A+B)；离散化所得算法包含经典方法及其惯性变体作为特例，并在稀疏信号恢复、图像去模糊等任务中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的二阶动力系统及对应的双惯性算法为求解一类广泛的结构化包含问题提供了新的理论框架和高效工具，展现出良好的收敛性质和应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel second-order dynamical system driven by a forward-backward splitting operator for solving the structured inclusion $0\in(A+B)(x)$ in a real Hilbert space, where $A$ is a maximal $ρ$-comonotone operator and $B$ is a $ν$-cocoercive operator. The well-posedness of the system is established, and Lyapunov analysis yields accelerated convergence rates of order $o(\frac{1}{t})$ for the velocity and $o(\frac{1}{t^2})$ for the forward-backward residual, together with weak convergence of the trajectories to $\operatorname{zer}(A+B)$. Temporal discretization further leads to a class of double inertial Halpern forward-backward splitting algorithms that encompasses the classical forward-backward splitting method and its inertial variants as special cases. Numerical experiments on split feasibility, sparse signal recovery, and image deblurring illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [168] [Learning Parametric Monotone Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02494)
*Alberto Bemporad,Tatiana Tatarenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了从数据中学习参数化纳什均衡（NE）问题的方法，确保在所有参数值下都具有单调性或强单调性，并提出了两种促进单调性的学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了能够在存在局部和共享凸约束的情况下高效计算广义纳什均衡，需要保证学习到的博弈问题是单调的。

Method: 提出了一种基于惩罚项的方法以及一种基于表示定理的参数化方法，后者能确保无论使用何种训练数据，所学博弈问题对所有参数值都是单调的。

Result: 通过多个数值例子展示了所提方法的有效性和适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的两种方法能够有效学习满足单调性的纳什均衡问题，且第二种方法能从根本上保证单调性。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning from data a parametric Nash equilibrium (NE) problem that is monotone (or strongly monotone) for all parameter values. In the presence of local and shared convex constraints, monotonicity enables efficient computation of generalized Nash equilibria of the learned game. We consider two learning scenarios: (i) direct learning of the NE problem from samples of the agents' costs, and (ii) inverse learning of surrogate agents' costs from samples of their best responses. We propose two methods to solve these tasks. The first is a penalty-based approach that promotes monotonicity of the learned game during training. The second, based on a representation theorem we introduce for a broad class of monotone games, parameterizes the agents' costs so that monotonicity of the NE problem is guaranteed by construction, for all parameter values, regardless of the training data used. We illustrate the applicability of the proposed methods on several numerical examples. A Python library and the examples reported in the paper are available at https://github.com/bemporad/learn_monotone_games.

</details>


### [169] [Projected Subgradient Methods for a Class of Nonsmooth and Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02517)
*Christian Kanzow,Jannis Krüger,Leo Lehmann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类在非光滑条件下满足下降引理的非凸优化问题，提出了一种带两种全局化策略的投影次梯度法，并证明了算法的收敛性，在MPEC、MAXCUT和鲁棒PCA等问题上取得了良好的数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非光滑、非凸且约束集非凸的优化问题，传统方法可能无法保证收敛到较强意义下的稳定点，因此需要设计新的算法以处理这类复杂结构（如稀疏或低秩）的问题。

Method: 提出一种投影次梯度法，结合两种全局化策略：(a) 非单调线搜索；(b) 自适应条件化方法，步长由历史迭代数据决定。适用于目标函数为upper-C²函数、约束可能诱导稀疏或低秩结构的情形。

Result: 所提两种方法均能收敛到比标准次微分和规则所给出的更强意义下的稳定解，并在MPEC风格问题、MAXCUT及鲁棒PCA等矩阵优化问题中展现出良好的数值性能。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了非光滑优化中下降类方法的应用范围，通过增强的全局化策略有效处理非凸约束与非光滑目标，为稀疏和低秩结构优化提供了理论支持与实用算法。

Abstract: We investigate the optimization problem of minimizing a nonsmooth function that satisfies a nonsmooth version of the descent lemma over a nonempty and closed but not necessarily convex set. The objective function belongs to the class of upper-$\mathcal{C}^2$ functions, whereas the constraints may promote a sparse or low-rank structure. We propose a projected subgradient method with two different globalization strategies: (a) a nonmonotone linesearch and, under additional assumptions, (b) an auto-conditioned method, where the stepsize is given by a formula depending on data from past iterations. We show that both methods converge to solutions that satisfy a stronger stationarity concept than one would expect from the subdifferential sum-rule, which is particularly important since the optimization problems of interest are inherently nonconvex. Finally, we present promising numerical results when applying the algorithm to an MPEC-style problem as well as the matrix optimization problems MAXCUT and Robust PCA.

</details>


### [170] [Insensitizing Control Problems for Coupled Stochastic Parabolic Systems with State and Gradient Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02712)
*Said Boulite,Abdellatif Elgrou,Abdelaziz Rhandi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了一类耦合线性随机抛物系统的不敏感控制问题，通过建立新的全局Carleman估计，证明了在不同权重参数下存在局部化控制使得哨兵泛函对初始数据的小扰动不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决耦合随机抛物系统中哨兵泛函对初始数据扰动的敏感性问题，确保观测的稳定性。

Method: 将不敏感控制问题重述为前向-后向随机抛物系统的零可控性问题，利用对偶性转化为伴随系统的可观测性不等式，并推导出带有零阶和二阶耦合项的全局Carleman估计。

Result: 在适当的几何假设下，建立了耦合随机抛物系统的新型全局Carleman估计，获得了所需的可观测性不等式，从而证明了不敏感控制的存在性；根据权重参数β的不同取值，确定所需控制的数量和位置。

Conclusion: 当β∈{0,1}时，仅需一个位于第一方程漂移项的局部控制；当β∈(0,1)时，需要两个分别作用于两个方程漂移项的局部控制，此外还有两个控制作用于扩散项。

Abstract: We study insensitizing control problems for a class of coupled linear stochastic parabolic systems. We establish the existence of controls such that a sentinel functional, involving localized observations of the state variables and their spatial gradients, is insensitive to small perturbations of the null initial data. We first reformulate the insensitizing control problem as a null controllability problem for a coupled forward--backward stochastic parabolic system, in which the observation terms induce both zeroth- and second-order coupling terms. By duality, the analysis is reduced to an observability inequality for the corresponding adjoint system. The main analytical contribution is the derivation of new global Carleman estimates for coupled stochastic parabolic systems with zeroth- and second-order coupling terms, under suitable geometric assumptions on the control and observation regions. These estimates yield the required observability inequalities and, consequently, the existence of insensitizing controls. Furthermore, depending on the value of a weighting parameter $β\in[0,1]$, which determines the relative contributions of the two state components to the sentinel functional, we consider two cases. If $β\in\{0,1\}$, the sentinel functional depends on only one state component, and a single localized control acting in the drift of the first equation is sufficient. In contrast, if $β\in(0,1)$, both state components contribute to the sentinel functional, and two localized controls acting in the drift terms of the two equations are sufficient. Moreover, the control strategy in this paper involves two additional controls acting throughout the diffusion terms.

</details>


### [171] [An Adaptive Projected-Gradient Algorithm for Sample-Average Approximations of Stochastic Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02722)
*Yiyang Li,Lei Wang,Xiaojun Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需线搜索和函数值计算的自适应投影梯度算法，用于随机多目标优化的样本平均近似问题，具有理论收敛保证并验证了在多个实际问题中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决随机多目标优化中仅能获得样本梯度信息、且传统方法依赖函数值或线搜索的问题，提升算法的实用性与鲁棒性。

Method: 设计了一种自适应正则化投影多梯度算法，利用投影步长自动更新正则化参数，并基于法锥条件构造下降估计，分析SAA问题的Pareto平稳性残差复杂度。

Result: 获得了SAA问题的显式复杂度界，证明了SAA残差趋于零时对应于总体问题的Pareto平稳性，并给出了紧集上的有限样本残差界；实验验证了算法在多个任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提算法无需线搜索和函数值评估，具备理论收敛性与良好的实践性能，适用于复杂的随机多目标优化问题。

Abstract: We consider stochastic multi-objective optimization over a nonempty closed convex set, where every objective is an expectation and only sample-gradient information is available. We develop a line-search-free and function-value-free adaptive projected-gradient algorithm for the sample-average approximation (SAA) problem. Each iteration computes a feasible regularized multi-gradient step and updates the regularization parameter from the projected step length. A normal-cone-based certificate yields descent estimates and an explicit complexity bound for the Pareto-stationarity residual of the SAA problem. The consistency of SAA gradients then transfers vanishing SAA residuals to Pareto stationarity for the population problem, while an additional concentration argument gives a finite-sample residual bound on compact sets. Experiments on synthetic problems, classification, portfolio selection, multi-task learning, and robot control illustrate the practical performance of our algorithm.

</details>


### [172] [Fundamental Limits of Adaptive Stabilization with an Unknown Growth Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02740)
*Zhaobo Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在离散时间非线性系统中，当系统的增长指数未知时，反馈控制的可稳定化临界指数如何变化。结果表明，当指数和系数均未知时，临界指数为$3\sqrt{3}/2$，低于已知指数情况下的4，并揭示了参数不确定性对系统稳定范围的本质影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在非线性系统参数（包括增长指数）未知的情况下，现有自适应控制理论中的稳定边界是否仍然适用，从而扩展对可稳定化条件的理解。

Method: 通过分析具有未知系数和增长指数的离散时间非线性系统的反馈稳定性，结合参数邻域与紧集假设，推导出新的临界指数，并比较不同参数不确定性结构下的稳定边界。

Result: 当增长指数未知时，可稳定化的临界指数为$3\sqrt{3}/2$；若指数属于已知有限集，则临界值仍为4；在指数略高于该阈值且参数函数雅可比满秩时，任意紧邻域均不可稳定。

Conclusion: 增长指数的不确定性会实质性改变可被反馈稳定的系统增长范围，而不仅仅是缩小可稳定参数集的大小，因此传统基于固定指数的分析需修正以适应更一般的未知情况。

Abstract: A basic question in adaptive control is how rapidly a discrete-time nonlinear plant with unknown parameters may grow while remaining stabilizable. When the nonlinear growth exponent is known and only a scalar coefficient is unknown, existing theory identifies 4 as the exact critical exponent for stabilizability. We determine how this critical exponent changes when the growth exponent is also unknown. For any positive disturbance bound, one feedback law stabilizes all plants in some neighborhood of a nominal parameter pair if and only if the nominal exponent is below $3\sqrt{3}/2$. At equality, every compact parameter set whose exponents do not exceed this value remains stabilizable. If the exponent belongs to a known finite set, the critical exponent remains 4 for every nondegenerate compact coefficient interval. Hence finite exponent sets and arbitrarily short exponent intervals can have different critical exponents. Uncertainty in the growth rate therefore changes the range of growth that feedback can stabilize, not merely the size of the uncertainty set. The critical exponent remains $3\sqrt{3}/2$ under a known positive state-dependent multiplier bounded above and away from zero. For a system whose coefficient and exponent are known functions of an unknown parameter, a compact parameter family is stabilizable when its exponents do not exceed this value. At an interior parameter point where the Jacobian of these functions has rank two and the exponent is at least this value, no compact neighborhood is stabilizable.

</details>


### [173] [Improved Gradient Descent Lower Bounds Beyond Nesterov](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02855)
*Yuhan Ye,Kaizhao Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在光滑凸优化中，通过预设步长加速梯度下降法的极限。提出了优于先前工作的非任意时间和任意时间下的下界，并与银调度实现的收敛率结合，建立了两种设置下可达到收敛指数的严格分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索在光滑凸优化问题中，使用预定步长策略对梯度下降进行加速所能达到的理论极限，超越经典的Nemirovsky和Yudin的一阶 oracle 下界。

Method: 通过理论分析证明了非任意时间与任意时间情形下的新下界，分别是Ω(n^{-1.6342})和Ω(n^{-1.2408})，并将其与已知的银调度所达成的上界进行比较。

Result: 得到了比现有结果更强的下界：非任意时间下为Ω(n^{-1.6342})，任意时间下为Ω(n^{-1.2408})，分别改进了Ma和Chen以及Tsai等人的结果；结合银调度的上界，证实了两类设定之间存在严格的收敛速率差异。

Conclusion: 在预定步长条件下，梯度下降的加速能力在任意时间与非任意时间设置中存在本质区别，本文的结果确立了这种严格分离。

Abstract: We study how far gradient descent (GD) can be accelerated by predetermined stepsizes in smooth convex optimization. Going beyond the classical $Ω(n^{-2})$ first-order oracle lower bound of Nemirovsky and Yudin, we prove an $Ω(n^{-1.6342})$ non-anytime lower bound and an $Ω(n^{-1.2408})$ anytime lower bound. These improve the recent $Ω(n^{-1.932})$ non-anytime lower bound of Ma and Chen and the $Ω(n^{-4/3})$ anytime lower bound of Tsai et al., respectively. Together with the non-anytime $O(n^{-\log_2(1+\sqrt{2})})$ rate achieved by silver schedules, our anytime lower bound establishes a strict separation between the achievable convergence exponents in the two settings.

</details>


### [174] [Gaussian-Restricted Barycenters for KL-Unbalanced Optimal Transport: Variational Theory and Fixed-Point Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02870)
*Jiaping Yang,Yunxin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了在无耦合熵的二次双侧Kullback-Leibler非平衡最优传输下，带有独立边缘惩罚的高斯限制性重心问题，提出了收敛的优化算法并分析了其在不同惩罚尺度下的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决非平衡最优传输中高斯分布重心计算的问题，尤其是在无耦合熵和独立边际惩罚条件下的建模与求解难点。

Method: 通过精确刻画重心质量将问题转化为具有内生Gibbs权重的光滑高斯形状泛函，建立全局存在性，推导稳态矩方程，并构造基于反向KL散度的MM迭代算法以保证收敛性。

Result: 证明了MM迭代序列从任意非退化高斯初始化出发均收敛至稳定不动点；发现了Bures-Wasserstein与Fisher-Rao度量之间的张量耦合关系；在相同惩罚尺度下，全局最小值收敛到具有效权重的高斯Wasserstein重心，且大惩罚时解唯一并可解析展开；小惩罚时，最小值趋近于加权Chernoff亲和力泛函的最大值集。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了对高斯限制性非平衡OT问题的系统分析，提供了有效的数值优化方法，并揭示了其在不同惩罚极限下的几何与渐近结构。

Abstract: We study Gaussian-restricted barycenters for quadratic two-sided Kullback--Leibler unbalanced optimal transport with independent marginal penalties and no coupling entropy. Exact profiling of the barycenter mass reduces the problem to a smooth Gaussian shape functional with endogenous Gibbs weights. We establish global attainment, derive the stationary moment equations, and construct a reverse-KL majorization--minimization (MM) iteration whose full sequence converges from every nondegenerate Gaussian initialization to a stationary fixed point. The diagonal second variation induces a parallel-sum tensor coupling the Bures--Wasserstein and Fisher--Rao metrics; its finite-mass extension admits a radial cone representation. Under common penalty scaling, global minimizers converge to a Gaussian Wasserstein barycenter with effective weights; for sufficiently large penalties, the minimizer is unique and admits a first-order analytic expansion. In the small-penalty regime, the distance of every global minimizer to the compact maximizer set of a weighted Chernoff affinity functional vanishes with respect to the mean--covariance parameter distance. Numerical experiments illustrate MM descent, local contraction, and the two penalty limits.

</details>


<div id='nlin.CD'></div>

# nlin.CD [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Basin Geometry and Reliable Recall of Dynamical Memories in Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01914)
*Ling-Wei Kong,Ying-Cheng Lai*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 揭示了储层计算中记忆吸引子的‘章鱼状’结构，通过同步机制克服不可预测性实现可靠回忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在复杂、破碎化的吸引域中如何实现可靠的记忆回忆问题。

Method: 分析储层计算和训练后的循环神经网络中的记忆盆地几何结构，研究线索驱动的广义同步机制。

Result: 发现记忆盆地呈‘章鱼状’结构，具有鲁棒的头部和细长缠绕的触手；触手区域初始状态导致极低不确定性指数；同步机制可引导系统进入鲁棒头部，建立最小线索时长、同步速率与盆地头半径之间的定量关系。

Conclusion: 该机制解释了即使在传统意义上不稳定的情况下也能实现可靠记忆回忆的原因，并表明这一现象可能普遍存在于多种神经网络模型中。

Abstract: Reliable attractor recall conventionally requires broad basins of attraction. However, in reservoir-computing based associative memory, temporal cues reliably recover dynamical memories despite basins dominated by unpredictable, riddled-like regions. We reveal that memory basins exhibit an ``octopus-like'' structure: a robust ``head'' near the attractor and thin, intertwined ``tentacles'' spanning state space. Initial states in tentacular regions yield near-zero uncertainty exponents, making the recalled memory effectively unpredictable at finite precision. Yet, cue-driven generalized synchronization bypasses this unpredictability, driving the system into the robust basin head. This mechanism yields a quantitative relation linking minimum cue duration, synchronization rate, and basin-head radius. Trained recurrent neural networks exhibit similar geometry, suggesting this phenomenon extends beyond reservoir computing.

</details>


### [176] [An active Lorentz gas: walking droplets in periodic media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02419)
*Aleksi Majaniemi,Esko Toivonen,Rahil N. Valani,Rainer Klages,Esa Räsänen*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种主动洛伦兹气体模型，通过数值模拟研究了耗散动力学对相空间结构的影响，揭示了稳定岛的坍缩、分岔级联、反常扩散等现象，为耗散系统中的输运研究提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从微观确定性混沌的角度理解非平衡输运的起源，并将活性物质与经典的混沌输运模型联系起来。

Method: 用光滑势代替传统洛伦兹气体中的硬盘，并引入具有非线性主动摩擦的行走液滴模型，在低记忆极限下构建主动洛伦兹气体；采用能量方差滤波方法区分不同类型的轨迹并构造非保守系统的分岔图。

Result: 发现主动动力学会使稳定岛坍缩为吸引和排斥集；识别出强分岔级联、反常扩散和显著的相空间收缩等参数区域；成功区分了局域周期、准弹道周期和混沌轨迹。

Conclusion: 主动洛伦兹气体为研究耗散动力系统中的输运提供了丰富框架，并建立了活性物质与经典混沌输运模型之间的桥梁，对周期介质中的流体动力学量子类比具有潜在意义。

Abstract: The Lorentz gas is a paradigmatic model in dynamical systems theory for understanding the origin of nonequilibrium transport in terms of microscopic deterministic chaos. In the periodic setting, a point particle scatters elastically off disks arranged on a two-dimensional lattice. Here we replace the disks by smooth potentials and the particle by the widely studied walking droplet, which propels itself on a vertically vibrating fluid. In the low-memory limit, this droplet reduces to a particle with nonlinear active friction. We call this system an active Lorentz gas. Using extensive numerical simulations, we analyze how dissipation generated by the active deterministic dynamics alters the phase-space structure of the corresponding conservative Lorentz gas. We find that islands of stability collapse into attracting and repelling sets. To characterize these structures, we introduce an energy-variance filtering method that distinguishes localized periodic, quasi-ballistic periodic, and chaotic trajectories, enabling the construction of bifurcation diagrams in a non-conservative setting. We identify parameter regimes exhibiting strong bifurcation cascades, anomalous diffusion, and significant phase-space contraction. Our results establish the active Lorentz gas as a rich framework for studying transport in dissipative dynamical systems and provide a bridge between active matter and classical models of chaotic transport, with potential implications for hydrodynamic quantum analogs in periodic media.

</details>

# nlin.AO [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Arithmetic of the sync basin for pulse-coupled oscillato](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01668)
*K. P. O'Keeffe*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了N个相同的脉冲耦合振荡器的同步行为，发现其最终状态（完全同步或簇状同步）由N的质因数分解决定。在临界充电曲线（线性）下，同步区域展现出精确的算术结构，且同步概率具有明确的数学表达式，揭示了一种新型的“算术型”吸引域几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大规模振荡器系统中同步与分簇现象的决定机制，并探索其背后的数学规律，尤其是与数论（如质因数分解）之间的联系。

Method: 通过分析脉冲耦合振荡器模型在临界线性充电曲线下的动力学行为，结合数论方法，推导出同步概率的精确递推关系和闭式解，并研究其随N的质因数特性变化的渐近行为。

Result: 发现了同步结果由N的质因数决定：当N为质数时，同步概率为1-1/N^N；当N为合数时，非同步概率按最小质因子m的幂律衰减。同步区域具有精确的算术结构，构成一种新的吸引域几何类型。

Conclusion: 振荡器系统的同步行为可由数论性质精确刻画，揭示了动力系统中存在‘算术型’吸引域，拓展了对复杂系统相空间结构的理解。

Abstract: A population of $N$ identical pulse-coupled oscillators ultimately settles into one of two outcomes: full synchrony or a state of co-existing synchronized clusters. We show that which outcome occurs is controlled by the prime factorization of $N$. At the critical charging curve --- linear, the boundary between the synchronizing and clustering regimes --- the synchronization basin acquires exact arithmetic structure. The synchronization probability is $\Psync=A_{N,1}/N^N$ for all $N$, where $A_{N,1}$ satisfies an exact recurrence relation. For prime $N$, $A_{N,1}=N^N-1$ giving the closed form $\Psync=1-1/N^N$; for composite $N$, the observed asymptotic scaling is $1-\Psync\sim C_m N^{-(m-1)}$, where $m$ is the smallest prime divisor. The result adds a new member to the atlas of exotic basin geometries: alongside fractal, riddled, and tentacled basins, we now have a basin that is arithmetic.

</details>


### [178] [Phase-delays shape multistability and basin sizes in Kuramoto networks: analytical estimates from network structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02047)
*Kalel L. Rossi,Antonio Mihara,Lyle E. Muller,Rene O. Medrano-T,Roberto C. Budzinski*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文研究了网络连通性和相位延迟如何共同影响有限振子网络的时空动力学，提出了一种结合连通性和相位延迟的复合矩阵谱理论，能够预测集体态的线性稳定性及吸引域大小，并揭示了传统稳定性分析无法发现的手性动力学和多稳态现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解网络连通性和异质相位延迟如何共同决定振子网络中选择何种相锁定态，解决现有理论在预测吸引域大小和复杂动力学方面的局限。

Method: 构建了一个融合网络连通性和相位延迟的复合矩阵，利用其谱特性分析集体态的线性稳定性与吸引域大小，并应用于非局域和全局耦合网络（包括随机相位延迟情况）。

Result: 发现复合矩阵的谱不仅决定集体态的线性稳定性，还决定了其吸引域大小；可仅从连通性和相位延迟解析估计个体网络中相锁定态的吸引域；发现了多稳态和吸引域的强不对称性，揭示出手性动力学。

Conclusion: 复合矩阵的谱是决定振子网络动力学行为的关键因素，该框架为理解和预测复杂网络中的相变和吸引域提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We study how network connectivity and heterogeneous phase-delays shape the spatiotemporal dynamics of finite oscillator networks. Phase-delays can destabilize global synchronization and promote phase-locked patterns, including states with uniform phase gradients and more complex combinations of these modes. Yet, how connectivity and phase-delays jointly determine which states the network selects remains unclear. Here, we show that the spectrum of a composite matrix, which combines connectivity and phase-delays, governs not only the linear stability of the network's collective states but also their basin sizes. This, in turn, enables analytical estimates of basin size of phase-locked states for individual networks from connectivity and phase-delays alone. Applying this framework to nonlocal and global networks, including cases with random phase-delays, we uncover multistability and strong asymmetries in basin sizes, revealing chiral dynamics that conventional stability analysis cannot detect.

</details>
